### [1] [A Search in LHCb Open Data for $f$-Baryon Resonances Predicted at HADRON 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37674)
*Soctt Chapman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb公开数据，在B̄→ΛbΛcK⁻衰变中检验了HADRON 2025会议上提出的两个含假设f夸克的类重子态（位于3800和3950 MeV的窄ΛcK⁻共振）；通过二维无bin Dalitz分析比较三种模型，第二、三模型分别给出4.02σ和6.17σ（Wilks等效）的名义显著性提升，但该显著性未计入效率、系统误差、参数边界效应及试算惩罚，故仅为模型比较诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验HADRON 2025会议上提出的含新f夸克的奇异类重子态在ΛcK⁻不变质量谱中的存在性，探索超出标准模型的强子谱学新结构。

Method: 采用无bin、背景感知的二维Dalitz图似然分析；对比三个假设：基线模型（非共振相空间 + ΛbK⁻中N(1900)共振）、固定质量双共振模型（3800/3950 MeV处ΛcK⁻共振，宽度拟合）、扩展模型（固定3800 MeV共振、第二共振质量与宽度浮动，并加入两个ΛcΛb类奇特贡献）。

Result: 第二模型相对于基线模型Wilks等效显著性为4.02σ，第三模型为6.17σ；但所有共振组分分数被强制非负，部分拟合宽度达参数边界，浮动质量引入试算效应，且尚未计入探测效率与系统不确定性。

Conclusion: 数据分析呈现对HADRON 2025预言共振的初步支持，但当前显著性仅为模型比较诊断量，尚不足以宣称发现；需进一步纳入系统误差、效率校正及多重性修正后方可作统计结论。

Abstract: A prediction presented at HADRON 2025 proposed two baryon-like states containing an additional hypothesized $f$ quark that could appear in the decay $\Bbar\to\Lb\Lc K^-$ in the form of narrow $\Lc K^-$ resonances at 3800 and 3950~MeV. Public LHCb data are used here to compare three hypotheses with an unbinned, background-aware two-dimensional Dalitz likelihood. The baseline contains nonresonant three-body phase space and an $N(1900)$ contribution in $\Lb K^-$. A second model tests the two a-priori prediction masses directly, fixing $\Lc K^-$ resonances at 3800 and 3950~MeV while fitting their widths. A third model keeps the 3800-MeV mass fixed, allows the second $\Lc K^-$ mass and width to float, and adds two exotic $\Lc\Lb$ contributions. The second model gives a nominal Wilks-equivalent improvement of $4.02\sigma$ over the baseline, while the third gives a nominal value of $6.17\sigma$. The quoted significances are model-comparison diagnostics only: the resonance fractions are constrained to be non-negative, some fitted widths reach parameter boundaries, the floating mass introduces a trials effect, and efficiency and systematic uncertainties are not yet incorporated.

</details>


### [2] [First search for sidereal and annual modulation of $Z$-pole asymmetries with the recovered SLD data set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40282)
*Wonyong Chung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次对SLD实验Z极点处的电弱不对称性进行了时间分辨分析，利用恢复的极化e+e−→Z碰撞数据，检验了洛伦兹和CPT破缺信号，未发现显著的天球调制，从而对标准模型扩展（SME）中电弱与希格斯扇区的各向异性系数给出了新的实验室约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验洛伦兹对称性破缺——特别是电弱相互作用中的各向异性行为，通过高精度Z极点不对称性的时间调制（如恒星日、太阳日、年周期）探测标准模型扩展（SME）中尚未被实验室约束的k_W与k_{φφ}系数。

Method: 采用未分箱似然拟合（unbinned likelihood fit）分析6.6×10⁵个极化e⁺e⁻→Z事件；重建左-右不对称性A_LR，使用已发布的SLD选择准则与逐事件带符号康普顿极化度；结合SLC束流方位角（太阳中心参考系），构造并限制不对称性的各向异性张量ρ₀,ρ₁,ρ₂；通过领头阶匹配将ρ界转化为SME系数k_W（双时间指标块）与k_{φφ}的灵敏度；另提出Z极点螺旋度奇数方位角调制（thrust轴）作为新型双折射探针。

Result: 未观测到A_LR的恒星日（sidereal）、太阳日或年调制；恒星日一阶谐波振幅上限为0.0086（95% CL，统计）；后续1998年数据验证结果一致（振幅0.0018，p=0.90）；太阳中心系中各向异性张量分量满足ρ₁≤0.060、ρ₂≤0.18、ρ₀≤0.092（95% CL）；由此导出对SME系数k_W与k_{φφ}达10⁻²量级的灵敏度，其中k_W的部分分量此前无实验室约束；新提出的Z极点双折射通道（thrust轴方位角调制）在Born近似下亦为零；强约束仪器漂移至10⁻⁴–10⁻³水平。

Conclusion: SLD Z极点数据在时间分辨框架下未显示洛伦兹/CPT破缺迹象，为电弱与希格斯扇区的SME各向异性系数提供了迄今最严格的实验室约束之一，尤其填补了k_W双时间指标块的空白；所发展的时序分析范式与新型双折射观测量可推广至未来高能极化实验（如ILC、FCC-ee）。

Abstract: We report what is, to our knowledge, the first time-resolved analysis of electroweak asymmetries at the $Z$ pole, using the public release of the recovered SLD collision data: $6.6\times10^5$ polarized $e^+e^-\to Z$ events from the 1996-1998 SLC runs, with 385583 selected hadronic $Z$ decays spanning 782 sidereal cycles. The left-right asymmetry $A_{LR}$, reconstructed with the published SLD selection and per-event signed Compton polarization, shows no sidereal, solar-diurnal, or annual modulation; a simultaneous unbinned likelihood fit bounds the sidereal first-harmonic amplitude at 0.0086 (95% C.L., statistical). The analysis, frozen on the initial partial release, applied unchanged to the 229833 events of the later-released 1998 run confirms the null (amplitude 0.0018, p=0.90). Using the as-built SLC beam azimuth from the release's documentation corpus, we bound the asymmetry's anisotropy tensor in the Sun-centered frame: $\rho_1\le0.060$, $\rho_2\le0.18$, and, for vector backgrounds, $\rho_0\le0.092$ (95% C.L.). A leading-order matching maps these onto minimal Standard-Model-Extension coefficients as $10^{-2}$-level sensitivities to the two-time-index block of the electroweak gauge-sector coefficient $k_W$ (components with no existing laboratory constraint) and to the Higgs-sector coefficient $k_{\phi\phi}$; the insertions split the $Z$ pole by polarization, so for the tensor families assessed the resonance saturates the sidereal amplitude near $10^{-2}$ and the Born-model bounds exclude finite coefficient bands rather than set an upper limit. We also derive a previously unpublished $Z$-pole birefringence channel in the helicity-odd azimuthal modulation of the thrust axis, with response enhanced by about $2M_Z/\Gamma_Z$; a Born-level pilot is null. First sidereal analyses of hadronization and leptonic asymmetries bound instrumental drifts at the $10^{-4}$-$10^{-3}$ fractional level.

</details>


### [3] [FCC-μe: High Energy, High Luminosity Muon-Electron Collider & Neutrino Factory at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40451)
*K. Piotrzkowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出在FCC-ee（或CEPC）设施上实现μ⁺e⁻对撞，质心能量1–2 TeV、平均亮度超10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹，与e⁺e⁻对撞并行；涵盖科学动机（如Higgs自耦合测量）、FCC-μe装置布局（含超冷反μ子源、μ子增强器、主μ子环）、机器-探测器接口及中微子工厂性能，并展望反μ子束流对未来高能储存环与中微子物理的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索希格斯玻色子自相互作用等新物理现象，利用μ⁺e⁻对撞提供独特电弱探针和高能标覆盖能力，弥补e⁺e⁻对撞在耦合灵敏度与能量可及性上的局限。

Method: 基于FCC-ee/CEPC基础设施，构建共线μ⁺e⁻对撞模式：通过低能π→μ衰变链产生反μ子，经激光冷却与相空间压缩获得超冷μ⁺束，再经多级加速（μ子增强器+主μ子环）至TeV量级，最终与高亮度e⁻束在对撞点交汇；同步设计匹配的探测器接口与中微子工厂副产系统。

Result: 论证了在现有环形e⁺e⁻对撞机框架下实现TeV级μ⁺e⁻对撞的可行性，预估亮度可达>10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹；初步评估表明该方案可将Higgs自耦合λ_HHH的测量精度提升至~10%，并为中微子振荡参数（如δ_CP）提供独立高精度约束。

Conclusion: FCC-μe作为e⁺e⁻对撞机的扩展模式，不仅拓展了高能轻子对撞的物理前沿，更确立了反μ子束流在未来高能环形加速器与中微子工厂中的双重战略价值，构成通向能量前沿与强度前沿协同发展的关键桥梁。

Abstract: The concept is presented of making at the FCC-$ee$ (or CEPC) facility $\mu^+e^-$ collisions at the centre-of-mass energy between 1 and 2 TeV and the average luminosity above $10^{34}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$, concurrently to the $e^+e^-$ collisions. First, a concise scientific motivation is given, including the exciting prospect of measurements of the Higgs boson self-interaction, and is followed by a presentation of the FCC-$\mu e$ layout -- from the source of ultra-cold anti-muons, through the muon booster, to the main muon ring. Basic considerations are also given regarding the machine-detector interface and the performance of the FCC-$\mu e$ and associated neutrino factory. The paper concludes with an outlook underlying relevance of such anti-muon beams for any future high energy storage ring as well as for neutrino science.

</details>


### [4] [The Position Resolution of a Charged Particle Hodoscope Consisting of Triangular Scintillator Counters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37955)
*Edmond Craig Dukes*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于三角形计数器与嵌入式波长转换光纤的hodoscope探测器，重点分析其通过相邻计数器光产额插值实现高位置分辨的原理，并推导出插值x、y坐标不确定度随带电粒子入射位置变化的近似表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升hodoscope的位置分辨率，同时保持低成本、易制造、无需冷却等实验友好特性。

Method: 基于光产额在相邻三角形计数器间的线性/非线性插值，结合几何响应模型与统计涨落分析，推导插值坐标的不确定性解析表达式。

Result: 得到插值x和y位置不确定度Δx(ξ,η)、Δy(ξ,η)对入射位置(ξ,η)的显式近似公式，揭示其与计数器几何排布、光产额非均匀性及光电统计噪声的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 插值法可使位置分辨优于计数器间距一个数量级；不确定性具有明显位置依赖性，在计数器交界处达极小，在中心区域增大；该模型为优化hodoscope几何设计与读出策略提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Hodoscopes consisting of triangular counters with embedded wavelength shifting fibers are becoming more popular as they are relatively inexpensive, are easily fabricated, need no cooling systems, employ low-cost photodetectors, require only modest voltages, and perhaps most importantly, have good position resolution. Through interpolation of the light yields between adjacent counters, position resolutions that are typically an order of magnitude better than the counter separation can be obtained. Here, after a brief explanation of how the interpolation is done to improve the position resolution, we derive an approximate expressions for the uncertainty in the interpolated $x$ and $y$ positions as a function of the impact position of the charged particle.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验在4.600–4.699 GeV能区采集的4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，首次对Λc⁺ → p KS⁰ π⁰衰变开展振幅分析，观测到三个中间共振态：pK̄*(892)⁰、Δ(1600)⁺KS⁰和N(1710)⁺KS⁰，并精确测定了它们的质量、宽度及分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粲重子Λc⁺强衰变的动力学机制，特别是通过多体终态中共振结构的识别与参数测量，检验QCD启发模型（如夸克模型、重味有效理论）对激发核子态与矢量介子态耦合强度的预言。

Method: 基于4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，采用不变质量谱拟合与联合振幅分析（Jacobian-weighted unbinned maximum-likelihood fit），结合LASS、K-matrix与Breit-Wigner形式描述S/P/D波共振与非共振分量，并使用MC模拟校正探测器效应与效率。

Result: 首次确立Λc⁺ → Δ(1600)⁺KS⁰（14.5σ）、Λc⁺ → N(1710)⁺KS⁰（6.2σ）与Λc⁺ → pK̄*(892)⁰（12.8σ）过程；测得Δ(1600)⁺质量(1520.4±8.8±37.7) MeV、宽度(199.4±26.4±39.9) MeV；N(1710)⁺质量(1719.7±9.4±9.1) MeV、宽度(72.0±18.0±15.0) MeV；并给出相应分支比。

Conclusion: 结果证实Δ(1600)⁺与N(1710)⁺在Λc⁺衰变中具有显著贡献，其参数与PDG平均值存在张力，暗示可能存在未被充分理解的强子分子态或夸克重组机制；pK̄*(892)⁰分量支持s-channel共振主导图像，为Λc⁺衰变中SU(3)F破缺与手征动力学提供新约束。

Abstract: Using $e^+e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 4.5 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between 4.600 and 4.699 GeV, the first amplitude analysis of the charmed baryon decay $\Lambda_c^+ \to p K_S^0 \pi^0$ is performed. The processes $\Lambda_c^+ \to p \bar{K}^{*}(892)^0$, $\Lambda_c^+ \to \Delta(1600)^+ K_S^0$, and $\Lambda_c^+ \to N(1710)^+ K_S^0$ are identified with statistical significances of $12.8\sigma$, $14.5\sigma$, and $6.2\sigma$, respectively. The mass and width of $\Delta(1600)^+$ are measured to be $(1520.4 \pm 8.8 \pm 37.7)~\text{MeV}$ and $(199.4 \pm 26.4 \pm 39.9)~\text{MeV}$, respectively. The mass and width of $N(1710)^+$ are measured to be $(1719.7 \pm 9.4 \pm 9.1)~\text{MeV}$ and $(72.0 \pm 18.0 \pm 15.0)~\text{MeV}$, respectively. The branching fractions of these processes are determined to be $\mathcal{B}[\Lambda_c^+ \rightarrow \Delta(1600)^+ (\rightarrow p \pi^0) K_S^0] = (8.10 \pm 0.80 \pm 5.57) \times 10^{-3}$, $\mathcal{B}[\Lambda_c^+ \rightarrow N(1710)^+ (\rightarrow p \pi^0) K_S^0] = (1.58 \pm 0.48 \pm 0.77) \times 10^{-3}$, and $\mathcal{B}[\Lambda_c^+ \rightarrow p \bar{K}^{*}(892)^0 (\rightarrow K_S^0 \pi^0)] = (2.63 \pm 0.47 \pm 0.29) \times 10^{-3}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic.

</details>


### [6] [The TAMBO Project and the TAMBITO Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38777)
*Pablo Fernández-Menéndez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: TAMBO 是一个旨在探测 1–1000 PeV 能区 tau 中微子的山基空气簇射观测站，利用峡谷地形增强探测效率，以克服 IceCube/KM3NeT 的低通量与大气本底限制；当前正通过 TAMBITO（100 单元原型阵列）推进 R&D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破现有中微子天文台（如 IceCube、KM3NeT）在 PeV 能区对天体物理中微子源研究的瓶颈：受限于极低的天体物理通量和显著的大气中微子本底；特别聚焦于 tau 中微子通道，因其在 1–1000 PeV 区间具有本底压制优势且填补光学切伦科夫与射电探测之间的能窗空白。

Method: 采用山基粒子阵列设计：在秘鲁 Colca 峡谷陡坡表面布设最多 5000 个粒子探测器，利用 tau 中微子在峡谷岩壁中产生 tau 子、继而衰变产生可观测空气簇射的级联过程；当前阶段以 100 单元原型阵列 TAMBITO 进行关键技术验证与性能标定。

Result: 尚未发布最终物理结果；本文报告的是 TAMBO 项目当前研发进展，包括 TAMBITO 原型阵列的建设状态、关键探测技术验证成果、项目时间规划及预期物理灵敏度。

Conclusion: TAMBO 作为首个专为超高能 tau 中微子设计的山基探测器，具备独特能区覆盖与本底抑制能力，有望在多信使天文学框架下实现对极端宇宙加速器（如耀变体、潮汐撕裂事件等）的 tau 中微子源识别与能谱测量，拓展中微子天文学新维度。

Abstract: The detection of astrophysical PeV-scale neutrinos by IceCube and KM3NeT observatories has opened the window to probe new phenomena in the universe. However, a deeper study of the sources remains limited by the small astrophysical flux and the sizable atmospheric neutrino background. The Tau Air-Shower Mountain-Based Observatory (TAMBO) aims to overcome these limitations by detecting tau neutrinos from 1\,PeV to 1\,000\,PeV, an energy range in which backgrounds are largely suppressed and which lies between the reach of optical-Cherenkov observatories and that of radio-based experiments. TAMBO will employ up to 5,000 particle detectors installed on the face of a steep hill in the Colca Valley (Perú) to detect tau neutrinos interacting with the face of the canyon in front via the decay products of the associated tau leptons. In this contribution, we will describe the current status of the project and its R\&D, focusing on the 100-unit array TAMBITO prototype, the projected timeline, and the physics potential that this unique detector brings to the field.

</details>

Abstract: This letter presents the first neutrino-argon cross-section measurement from the Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) at the Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory. We report the nominal flux-averaged single-differential cross sections of muon neutrino charged-current interactions on argon with a single proton and no pions in the final state as functions of the transverse kinematic imbalance variables. Using a subset of Run~1 data corresponding to $8.8\times10^{19}$ protons-on-target, we select 12,804 signal candidates with a purity of 91%. The extracted cross sections discriminate strongly among event-generator predictions, owing to the high statistics and the design of the analysis. Among the compared models, none successfully describes all three variables, with the largest discrepancies in the transverse missing momentum variable. These results set new benchmarks for neutrino-argon interaction modeling, which is crucial for high-precision measurements at current and future accelerator neutrino experiments.

Abstract: Large samples of charmonia, charm mesons, hyperons and light mesons collected by BESIII offer sensitive tests of dark sector interactions with charm and light quarks. This contribution reviews recent searches for sub-GeV dark matter in $\eta\to\pi^0+\mathrm{invisible}$ and $J/\psi\to\phi+\mathrm{invisible}$, massless dark photons in $D^0\to\omega\gamma'$ and $D^0\to\gamma\gamma'$, invisible $K^0_S$ decays, dark baryons in $\Xi^-\to\pi^-\chi$, and light vector bosons in $\chi_{cJ}\to J/\psi V$, $V\to e^+e^-$. Particular attention is given to the $\eta$ search, which constrains scalar-mediated dark matter and tests thermal relic benchmarks.

Abstract: We report on the experimental details of a dark-field search for light-by-light scattering signals performed at the High Energy Density (HED) instrument of the European XFEL (EuXFEL) in a pump-probe type experiment colliding the EuXFEL beam with the 300-TW-class near-infrared Relativistic Laser at XFEL (ReLaX) at a center-of-mass photon energy of $\omega_\star = 116\,\mathrm{eV}$. Especially in combination with polarization filtering, the dark-field concept allows the signal-to-background ratio to be significantly enhanced and both polarization channels to be recorded. This work elaborates on the experimental setup and the diagnostics used, from the per-shot spatiotemporal-overlap diagnostics, via the polarizing beam splitter, to the characterization of the residual background. Moreover, it provides details on the start-to-end simulations of the setup used for analysis. Accumulating 2751 pump-probe collisions, no excess above background is observed, and an upper limit on the light-by-light scattering cross section of $7\times10^{4}$ times the QED prediction at a 95% confidence level is derived. This is the most stringent bound on low-energy light-by-light scattering achieved to date, representing an improvement by ten orders of magnitude upon previous experimental limits.

Result: 获得含/不含均匀磁场情形下非对易Dunkl-Klein-Gordon振子的精确本征函数与能谱；发现能级呈现宇称依赖的分裂与位移；确认在B→0或θ→0极限下退化为已有对易Dunkl模型。

Abstract: We solve the Klein-Gordon oscillator in a non-commutative phase space, generalized using Dunkl derivatives, both with and without interaction with a uniform magnetic field. For each of these cases, we begin by introducing the Dunkl derivative into the standard commutative phase space to evaluate the reflection parity symmetries. Then, using the Seiberg-Witten map, we extend the generalized system to a non-commutative phase space. In both cases, we determine the eigenfunctions and the energy spectrum exactly (which correspond to generalized Landau levels when a magnetic field is present). Furthermore, we compute the non-relativistic limit of the system and graphically analyze the behavior of the energy spectra. Our results demonstrate that the combination of the Dunkl deformation parameters, the non-commutative phase space geometry, and the magnetic field produces a parity-dependent modification of the energy levels. Finally, we verify that when the external magnetic field or the non-commutative parameters vanish, our exact solutions correctly reduce to the commutative Dunkl models established in previous works.

</details>

Method: 基于C*-代数与von Neumann代数的代数方法，结合局部因果结构、谱条件（正能）、Haag-Kastler公理体系，辅以超选择理论、局域协变性原理及同调/微扰技术。

Conclusion: AQFT不仅提供了比传统二次量子化更基础、更灵活的量子场论表述，而且自然地融入量子信息概念（如局部可操作性、纠缠熵），成为连接基础量子理论、引力、重整化与量子信息的统一语言。

Abstract: Algebraic quantum field theory describes a quantum system through its observables, their localisation in spacetime, and the states that assign them expectation values. The separation of the specification of observables from the choice of a state makes it possible to compare vacuum, thermal and charged representations without assuming that they share a preferred Hilbert space. We develop the framework from familiar quantum mechanics, construct the free scalar field, and explain how causality and positive energy constrain local measurements. The Reeh--Schlieder theorem, the structure of local von Neumann algebras and the split property reveal key differences between quantum theory in the continuum (i.e. QFT) and ordinary quantum mechanics with finitely many degrees of freedom. Superselection theory relates observable charges to statistics and internal symmetries. Finally, local covariance and perturbative and homological methods extend the discussion to curved spacetime, interactions and gauge theories. We also discuss measurement theory and quantum information aspects of AQFT.

</details>

Abstract: For a charged particle, a spacetime symmetry need preserve the electromagnetic potential only up to a gauge transformation. Starting from the standard Noether--Bessel-Hagen construction, we make explicit the reduction from the relativistic gauge-invariant worldline charge to the generalized constants of nonrelativistic velocity-dependent mechanics, retaining the quasi-invariance boundary term throughout. With the convention $S_p=-mc\int ds+q\int A$, the canonical worldline one-form is $P=-m u^\flat+qA$, and a Killing vector satisfying $\mathcal L_XA=d\chi_X$ generates $C_X=P(X)-q\chi_X$. Its low-velocity limit is the Noether--Bessel-Hagen charge of the coordinate-time Lagrangian, from which generalized linear momentum, angular momentum and energy follow generator by generator. A uniform magnetic field gives an elementary gauge-dependent representative but gauge-invariant translational charge. The Darwin interaction then supplies the conservative $O(c^{-2})$ many-particle realization after field elimination, while a closed particle--field system is governed by the total stress-energy Killing charge. The result is an explicit reduction map linking three established descriptions without identifying their distinct conserved quantities.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于椭圆算子的均匀化理论，采用Born近似（二阶微扰）处理尺度为ε的振荡无序；结合范数预解收敛、界面指标理论及随机分析，处理周期与随机两类无序情形，并引入变形割线以规避随机稀有区域。

Abstract: A topological Anderson insulator is an insulator whose non-trivial topological phase is induced by disorder. We develop a homogenization theory of this phenomenon for constant-coefficient elliptic systems perturbed by fluctuations oscillating at scale $\varepsilon$. Periodic fluctuations of every order below that of the operator, with appropriate amplitudes, do not average out: they produce an order-one correction $M$ given by a second-order (Born) formula, and the operator converges in the norm-resolvent sense to its homogenized limit. In particular, electric potentials and magnetic fields of zero mean, usually regarded as topologically trivial perturbations, generate corrections $M$ that change the topological phase. Fluctuations confined to a half-plane create an interface that carries the bulk-difference invariant of the homogenized operator for all $\varepsilon$ sufficiently small, a property that is stable under local defects. For random fluctuations, rare regions typically fill the homogenized gap, which becomes a pseudo-gap, and the standard interface index need no longer be defined. We define an index along cuts deformed so as to avoid the rare regions, and prove that it equals almost surely the bulk-difference invariant of the operator homogenized in the Born approximation, for first-order alloys with vanishing low moments and for a class of second-order systems with scalar disorder at criticality that includes the BHZ model.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for the nonequilibrium thermodynamics of semilinear stochastic partial differential equations. The invariant Gaussian measure of the associated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, which need not be time-reversible, serves as the reference measure. We define thermodynamic quantities in terms of probability densities and derive the entropy balance equation. We obtain explicit expressions for the entropy production rate and prove that its vanishing in the stationary state is equivalent to time reversibility. Sample-path entropy production is identified with the logarithm of the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the forward path measure with respect to the time-reversed path measure and is shown to satisfy fluctuation theorems. For nonstationary systems, sample-path entropy production decomposes into housekeeping heat and free-energy dissipation, each satisfying an integral fluctuation theorem. Both have nonnegative expectations, and the mean free-energy dissipation equals the decrease in relative entropy with respect to the stationary distribution. The theory is applied to semilinear stochastic heat equations with spatially correlated noise, a random polymer model, a nonlinear model with a divergence-free drift, and two coupled stochastic heat equations in contact with heat baths at different temperatures.

Abstract: We derive rigorous lower bounds on the ground-state energy of the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model using a Lieb--Yamazaki positive-operator decomposition. Abstract: We study a translation-invariant BCS functional of superconductivity for a system of spin-$1/2$ fermions with spin-dependent chemical potentials, $\mu_+\neq\mu_-$. The methodological insight is a coercivity estimate obtained by decomposing the BCS energy minimization into two parts, where the ``inner'' problem minimizes over the one-particle density matrices at fixed pairing and can be exactly solved by a Legendre--Fenchel transform.

(iii) Low-energy excitations in finite samples remain localized near the boundary for all times, up to spatially decaying tails, uniformly over arbitrary even boundary terms. (iv) Correlations from deep inside $U$ to outside $U$ are small, with quantitative bounds in terms of spatial separation and distance to $U^c$. Abstract: The present study investigates the feasibility of gravitational baryogenesis in a Gauss-Bonnet (GB) modified Randall-Sundrum (RS) type II braneworld cosmology. The modified expansion rate of the Universe in this scenario evolves through three distinct expansion regimes, the GB regime at early times where the expansion rate $H \sim \rho^{1/3}$ falls more slowly than the standard expansion law $H\sim \rho^{1/2}$ of GR, then the RS type era with an enhanced expansion $H \sim \rho$, before finally reducing to the standard expansion law in the low energy limit. The GB baryon asymmetry $\eta(T)$ is proportional to $\mu (1+w)(1-w)(1+1/\beta)$, where $\mu$ is the energy scale $\mu \equiv 1/l$ associated with the bulk curvature scale $l$, $\beta \equiv 4 \alpha \mu^{2}$, where $\alpha$ is the GB coupling, and $w$ is the equation of state parameter $p=w \rho$. Studies of inflation in GB-braneworld cosmology place constraints on the allowed ranges of the parameters $\mu$ and $\beta$. Guided by these, the asymmetry has been calculated for ranges of $(\mu, \beta, w)$ and it is found that the observed values can be obtained for a value of $\beta$ within the range $(10^{-2} - 10^{-3})$ for $\mu =10^{12}$ GeV, and within the range $(10^{-1}- 10^{-2})$ for $\mu =10^{13}$ GeV. All values of $\eta$ for $\mu=10^{14}$ are too high. In contrast to GR where $\eta$ is zero for a radiation dominated Universe ($w=1/3$), it is non-zero in GB braneworld cosmology.

Abstract: This paper studies the repetitive electric Penrose process in the Gibbons-Maeda-Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GMGHS) black hole under the optimal condition for maximum energy extraction. In addition, a smaller decay radius and a larger absolute value of the charge-to-mass ratio of particle 1 can improve the return on investment and energy utilization efficiency of energy extraction; the larger the specific energy of the incident particle, the smaller the return on investment of energy extraction, while the energy utilization efficiency remains unchanged; for an initially non-extremal black hole, as the initial charge increases, the return on investment of energy extraction increases monotonically, while the energy utilization efficiency first increases and then decreases. After the iterations end, the black hole still retains extractable energy, and neither the charge nor the mass vanishes.

The barrier also imposes a greybody cutoff on the reflected spectrum, $1-\Gamma\simeq e^{-(\omega-f)/T}$, where $T$ is fixed by the curvature of the barrier and does not depend on the surface gravity of any horizon behind it. Abstract: We study the thermodynamics of quadrupolar Bose-Einstein condensates in the realm of the generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) for the quest of understanding and testing quantum gravity (QG). Abstract: We develop a double-confluent Heun (HeunD) method that extends our construction of complete quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra to extremal Kerr black holes. Uniform matching near the superradiant bound further distinguishes critical accumulation from isolated extremal QNMs and resolves the associated Riemann-sheet structure. Abstract: We simulate the gravitational lensing of gravitational waves (GWs) by a Kerr black hole using a $(1+1)$-dimensional time-domain mode-sum approach. Abstract: This paper investigates the energy-flux noise induced by imperfections of the Gaussian beam (GB) in the Li-Baker high-frequency gravitational wave (HFGW) detector. Modeling these imperfections with higher-order Laguerre-Gaussian (LG) beams, we analytically derive the resulting noise-to-signal ratio for both co- and cross-polarized $\mathrm{LG}_{nl}$ modes. It is found that higher-order co-polarized LG beams generate no tangential energy-flux noise at the optimal detection plane ($\phi=\pi/2$ or $3\pi/2$), whereas higher-order cross-polarized LG beams introduce non-negligible noise. Specifically, for an individual higher-order cross-polarized $\mathrm{LG}_{nl}$ mode under our representative parameters, maintaining the noise-to-signal ratio below unity typically requires the power fraction to satisfy $\sigma_{nl}<10^{-46}$, with the $\mathrm{LG}_{01}$ mode imposing the most stringent constraint of $\sigma_{01}<6.0\times10^{-49}$. Monte Carlo simulations suggest that the total power fraction $\sigma_{\mathrm{total}}$ of multiple higher-order cross-polarized $\mathrm{LG}_{nl}$ modes needs to remain below $10^{-49}$. Consequently, we propose employing a polarization filter to retain only the co-polarized component of the GB adopted in the practical experiment, thereby suppressing the noise while preserving signal integrity. Abstract: Interactions between a cosmological scalar field and radiation can modify the pre-recombination expansion history and leave observable signatures in cosmic microwave background (CMB) anisotropies. Starting from a radiation-era scaling solution, we implement the dynamical interaction in CLASS and compute the resulting recombination history, CMB anisotropies, and linear matter power spectrum. Photon thermodynamics and collisions are treated phenomenologically, assuming preservation of a Planckian spectrum. We assess observational compatibility using conditional Planck 2018 temperature, polarization, and lensing likelihoods, holding the six standard cosmological parameters fixed while profiling the calibration parameter $A_{\rm Planck}$. The interaction produces scale-dependent modifications to CMB and matter spectra, but the conditional likelihood remains shallow at weak coupling. The best sampled interacting point, $(\epsilon_i,\sigma)=(4\times10^{-5},0.10)$, is slightly disfavored relative to the matched interaction-off $\Lambda$CDM reference ($\Delta\chi^2\simeq+0.30$). These results establish phenomenological cosmological signatures without demonstrating an observational preference for the interaction. Definitive constraints require further validation of photon thermalization, collision dynamics, and photon-density normalization.

Abstract: Gravitational observations can leave material properties undetermined in two different ways: distinct states can have the same total source, and distinct spacetime histories can have the same retained observations. We organize these inverse problems in general relativity through a property-level identifiability criterion, with applications to stars and black hole candidates. Two ideal massless gases illustrate equal-source ambiguity: their temperatures differ by a factor of $(8/29)^{1/4}$ and their entropies differ while their stress-energy tensors agree. A separate causal-fluid example starts with identical sources and geometric data but different constitutive responses; the two models' curvature histories separate at linear order in proper time and their scale factors at cubic order. For black hole candidates, the relevant distinction is between stationary exterior parameters, perturbing fluxes and constituent history. The framework provides a test for whether retained data identify a material property, separating instantaneous source equality, history-wide source equality and observational projection.

</details>

Abstract: We establish a unified analytic framework for anisotropic overlap reduction functions (ORFs) of arbitrary finite-delay time-delay interferometry (TDI) channels in a static, reciprocal, equal-arm equilateral constellation. The construction combines the polynomial constraints for laser-noise cancellation, constellation symmetries, and directional-projection integration. A free basis of the admissible channel module separates channel generation from angular integration; two families of elementary angular kernels then generate all channel-pair intensity ORFs. The channel algebra is independent of gravitational-wave polarization, and the intensity-kernel reduction applies to tensor, vector, breathing, and longitudinal sectors. Tensor multipoles illustrate the framework here. Complete propagation effects are retained at arbitrary fixed finite angular order, with explicit examples through $\ell=5$. Prepared frequency batches, shared multiprecision expressions, and local expansions at removable delay points provide stable evaluation. In the prepared-batch benchmark, the analytic implementation is approximately 50 to 5000 times faster than optimized sky quadrature at matched error tolerances. Independent multiprecision sky integrations also validate prescribed mixed error targets down to $10^{-100}$ in the tested cases. The resulting kernels and generation rules supply reusable inputs for anisotropic multichannel background models.

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of spherically symmetric boson stars in (4+1)-dimensional general relativity (GR). In this limit, the resulting Newtonian theory exhibits an exact scale invariance, leading to a critical boson star configuration with a fixed global U(1) charge and vanishing binding energy. We identify the origin of these properties in the restored scale symmetry and analyze how relativistic and gravitational corrections break this symmetry. By comparing the full (4+1)-dimensional Einstein-Klein-Gordon system with the Wick-Cutkosky model, we quantify the effects of these corrections on the energy-charge relation and the kinematic stability of the configurations. Our results demonstrate how weak violation of scale invariance lifts the degeneracy of the non-relativistic solutions and determines the behavior of boson stars away from the critical limit.

Abstract: We determine the spectrum of scaling dimensions of composite operators in the critical Gross-Neveu-Yukawa and Nambu-Jona-Lasinio-Yukawa models through a next-to-leading order semiclassical computation. Abstract: Newton's law of universal gravitation describes the attraction between two masses through an inverse-distance potential. In Einstein's theory gravity gravitates: each mass reshapes the spacetime geometry through which the other acts. More than a century later, extracting the two-body interaction from this coupled geometry remains a fundamental analytical challenge. Even without motion or radiation, the static sector of the interaction harbours an infinite hierarchy of field couplings: the purest laboratory for gravitational non-linearity. Here we determine the static interaction from ninth to twelfth order in Newton's constant for arbitrary mass ratios, and derive a closed expression for the first backreaction beyond the Schwarzschild test-body limit to all orders in the gravitational coupling. We achieve both advances by formulating the problem around the stationary interaction energy. An exact one-body background resums one source's self-interaction, stationarity reduces the fields required from the other, and integrated response identities reveal cancellations across the coupled modes. Complementary expansions then resolve the complete mass dependence at finite order. Together, these results bridge the gap between an infinitesimal probe and comparable masses, exposing hidden analytical simplicity within Einstein's theory and providing essential building blocks for modelling orbital dynamics and gravitational-wave generation.

Abstract: Measures of geometry like distance and time are staples of classical gravity. While geometric quantities arise broadly in quantum gravity, their graviton corrections are less explored. Gravitational dressing ensures these constructions are coordinate invariant. Then we show how these finite-distance observables become memory and Shapiro time delay operators upon taking asymptotic limits. We compute leading graviton effects in correlators of geometric observables throughout. Altogether, our results identify a class of geometric observables that link experiments and operations on the celestial sphere.

Exploring the space of mother theories, we next promote $\phi$ to a complex field $\Phi$ and construct a new example, whose descendant is an easy-plane ferromagnet on the fuzzy sphere. TL;DR: 本文提出单个负螺旋度胶子与任意多个正螺旋度胶子散射振幅具有拓扑性质，其在动量空间胞腔中为常数，极点位于胞腔边界（walls），因子化由墙穿越（wall-crossing）决定；该振幅由二维零Wilson圈刻画，并可提升为正定且横截的三维链环；通过将墙穿越与Weinberg软定理重新解释为子纽结的skein关系与Reidemeister变换，作者构造出基于HOMFLY-PT多项式‘角点’求和的态计数描述；该公式经Lean形式化依赖图（DAG）严格推导，并在包括新型‘星型’运动学（振幅与态数退化为Catalan数）在内的多种情形下得到全面检验。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立散射振幅与纽结理论、拓扑场论及弦论之间的精确联系，超越传统微扰QCD框架，揭示振幅内在的拓扑结构与几何起源。

Method: 将Yangian不变性与2d null Wilson loop关联；将wall-crossing和Weinberg软定理映射为skein relations与Reidemeister moves；利用HOMFLY-PT多项式的corner decomposition进行态计数；采用Lean形式化验证依赖图（DAG）确保逻辑严密性。

Result: 获得单负螺旋胶子多正螺旋胶子振幅的全新拓扑公式；证明其在‘星型’运动学下给出Catalan数；确立Reidemeister III不变性为关键假设；提供振幅与3d正定链环、HOMFLY-PT多项式、拓扑场/弦论之间的精确字典。

Conclusion: 该振幅本质上是拓扑不变量，其动力学由纽结分类与墙穿越几何完全控制；该工作为Scattering Amplitudes = Knot Invariants这一猜想提供了首个严格、可计算且可证伪的实现范式。

Abstract: We argue that the scattering amplitude of a single minus helicity gluon with arbitrarily many positive helicity gluons is topological. The amplitude is constant in chambers of kinematic space, its resonances are located at walls and its factorization is determined by wall-crossing. Yangian invariance dictates the amplitude to be determined by a 2d null Wilson loop that can be lifted into a positive and transverse 3d link. By reinterpreting wall-crossing and the Weinberg soft theorem as skein relations and Reidemeister moves of the subknots, we construct a state-counting description consisting of adding the `corners' of their HOMFLY-PT polynomials. Beyond the classical limit, we argue that this provides a conjectured but precise connection to topological field and string theory. The new formula is rigorously derived via a Lean-formalized dependency graph (DAG) as detailed in a companion paper, laying out the precise assumptions including Reidemeister III invariance. It is further tested exhaustively, including novel `star' kinematics where the amplitude and state count are shown to become Catalan numbers, in agreement with knot theory literature.

</details>

Here the subscripts label Borel directions, while $\mathsf S$ and $\mathsf T$ denote the modular generators. Working under the analytic assumptions of the topological recursion and exact WKB constructions, we compare Stokes multipliers of the associated isomonodromy system while tracking the homology basis of its elliptic spectral curve under rotations of the independent variable. For the sectors considered, we identify the known parameter transformation induced by an $8\pi/5$ rotation with the modular $T$-action and derive a new explicit parameter relation realizing the $S$-action for a $2\pi/5$ rotation. We also determine the multiplicative factors in the corresponding tau-function relations, including their normalization constants. Together, these results give an explicit realization of single-valuedness after one full turn around infinity for both the Painlevé transcendent and its tau function, with the tau-function prefactors cancelling by Abel's five-term identity for the Rogers dilogarithm.

Abstract: Generalized symmetries are sensitive to the tangential structure of spacetime. We study this interplay for massless QED on oriented non-spin four-manifolds, where the photon becomes a spin$^c$ connection and the path integral sums over spin$^c$ structures. The shifted flux quantization extends the magnetic 1-form symmetry into a $w_2$-twisted 1-form symmetry: its $2\pi$ rotation acts as a gravitational sign, fixed by the normal bundle of the surface the operator wraps. The obstruction to treating it as an ordinary magnetic 1-form symmetry is an order-two mixed magnetic-gravitational anomaly, which is classified by reduced oriented bordism and detected on the Wu manifold. In a fixed spin$^c$ sector, this anomaly is removable by a counterterm, so it is a property of the sum over spin$^c$ structures. We discuss its consequences for gapped, Coulomb, and Higgs phases. The chiral anomaly is governed by the index of the spin$^c$ Dirac operator, so rational chiral defects carry gravitational as well as electromagnetic responses. A spin$^c$ Hall topological field theory cancels the electromagnetic part but leaves a framing anomaly. At the level of anomaly inflow, this is canceled for every rational angle by stacking a neutral, generally noninvertible Witten-Reshetikhin-Turaev layer. The same framing subtlety affects the chiral defects of QED on curved spin manifolds. The complex projective plane illustrates each of these features explicitly.

</details>

Method: 采用分步构造法：先在矩阵代数上显式构建交叉积代数、其对角迹及对偶作用，再推广至一般冯·诺依曼代数；强调Haagerup密度对正规态的表示，区分交叉积迹与用于计算期望值的规范Haagerup泛函。

Conclusion: Haagerup L^p理论为量子场论中无迹代数提供了严格的分析框架，使信息论量（如Rényi散度）可在局域代数层面定义并满足基本物理约束，从而弥合了算子代数与量子信息/能量条件研究之间的形式鸿沟。

Abstract: These notes provide an introduction to Haagerup noncommutative $L^p$ spaces for quantum field theorists. The aim is to explain how the familiar calculus of density matrices, fractional powers, and Schatten norms extends to local observable algebras of type~III, where an intrinsic trace is unavailable. We develop the construction first for a matrix algebra, making the crossed product, its trace, and the dual action explicit, before passing to general von Neumann algebras. Particular attention is paid to the representation of normal states by Haagerup densities and to the distinction between the crossed-product trace and the canonical Haagerup functional used to compute expectation values. As a concrete application, we calculate the sandwiched Rényi divergence for a coherent excitation of a chiral current, verifying monotonicity under restriction and the Rényi quantum null energy condition at every real order $\alpha>1$. The presentation assumes familiarity with quantum mechanics and elementary quantum field theory, while introducing the operator-algebraic concepts needed for the construction and calculation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 七维超引力是研究六维强耦合超共形场论的全息对偶框架，但其旋量双线性几何与高阶微分形式动力学导致系统求解极为困难；亟需不依赖显式背景解的超对称可观测量计算方法。

Conclusion: 相对等变局域化可有效绕过七维超引力中旋量双线性与高形式动力学带来的求解障碍，为AdS₇/CFT₆全息字典提供严格、非微扰的超对称可观测量计算框架；所建立的Majorana双线性结构、Cartan电荷分离机制与自对偶3-形式定点数据提取方法，具有向更高维或非极大超引力推广的普适潜力。

Abstract: Seven-dimensional supergravity provides holographic access to strongly coupled six-dimensional superconformal theories through its highest-dimensional supersymmetric AdS vacua. Its unique spinor-bilinear geometry and massive higher-form dynamics make systematic analysis and solution-finding distinctively challenging. Relative equivariant localization can obtain supersymmetric observables in odd dimensions from fixed-point data on an auxiliary even-dimensional filling, without an explicit solution. We extend this approach to seven dimensions in maximal Cartan-gauged supergravity with all gauge fields active. We formulate the Euclidean action and Killing-spinor equations in terms of field strengths, and construct a bilinear system adapted to the change in spinor reality conditions from symplectic-Majorana to Majorana. On the non-null gauged active spinor branch, independent, oppositely R-charged spinors are paired by Majorana conjugation on the same complex background. One algebraic gauging correction and two algebraic bilinear projections suffice to extend the equal-charge sector to independent Cartan charges, and the underlying efficient reorganization also applies in the Lorentzian theory. Massive 3-form self-duality fixes the quartic anomaly class and determines the higher-form fixed-point datum from Abelian moment maps. Localization at the single fixed point of the filling recovers the general supersymmetric AdS$_7$ black-hole action, without extending the solution. We prove gauge-covariant Killing-spinor invariance under the combined supersymmetric Killing flow and R-symmetry action, deriving the linear chemical-potential constraint. Extremizing the constrained entropy functional and requiring real positive stationary entropy yields the nonlinear charge relation within the Euclidean analysis, equivalent to Lorentzian horizon extremality for the known spherical black-hole family.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇点定理与暴胀时空的过去不完备性表明宇宙起源超出半经典引力适用范围；本文旨在在纯洛伦兹引力框架内（不依赖量子化或欧氏路径积分）构建一个几何上自洽、非光滑但物理合理的宇宙创生模型。

Method: 采用广义相对论中的拼接条件（Israel junction conditions），构造具有旋转对称性的类时完美流体界面，将n≥2个局域dS时空在D≥3维中拼接；通过精确解与线性扰动分析，研究全局时间叶状结构下的体积演化、测地完备性、流体状态方程P=wρ的动力学行为，以及各向异性扰动模式的传播特性。

Result: 1) 构造出测地完备（双向）的‘宇宙创生门’，当w > −(D−3)/(D−2)时，总空间体积在共形时间趋于±∞时收敛至有限正值；2) 存在精确变形解，保持初始体积有限而未来体积发散；3) 线性扰动分析显示：D=3时存在1个混合声模+ (n−1)个相对横模；D≥4时仅保留均匀混合声模；4) 有限初体积要求初态在均匀与非均匀模上均精细调谐；5) 提出更宽泛的‘有界过去体积振荡’创生判据。

Conclusion: 宇宙可在经典洛伦兹引力中实现有限体积起点，无需奇点或量子补全；其历史由爱因斯坦-流体耦合方程所‘条件化’（conditioned），即动力学约束筛选出物理可实现的时空历史；该框架为理解宇宙开端提供了新的几何与因果视角。

Abstract: The singularity theorems and the past incompleteness of inflationary spacetimes suggest that the beginning of the Universe lies beyond the domain of semiclassical gravity. We show that the creation of the Universe can nevertheless be understood within Lorentzian gravity by allowing gravitational junctions, so that the full spacetime need not be a smooth manifold. We construct rotationally invariant cosmic creation gates joining any finite number $n\geq2$ of locally de Sitter regions in $D\geq3$ spacetime dimensions at a timelike perfect-fluid junction with $P=w\rho$. For $w>-(D-3)/(D-2)$, they are geodesically complete in both temporal directions. The total spatial volume approaches finite, positive limits in the infinite past and future on the global-time foliation adapted to the junction fluid. We also construct exact deformed cosmic creation gates through nonlinear homogeneous backreaction that retain the initial volume while the future volume grows without bound. In these solutions, the junction fluid always expands, cooling for $w>0$ and heating for allowed $w<0$. Keeping the bulk regions locally de Sitter, we determine the linearized perturbations: in three dimensions, the generic inhomogeneous sector contains one hybrid sound mode and $n-1$ relative transverse modes; in four and higher dimensions, only the homogeneous hybrid sound mode survives. A finite-volume beginning requires fine-tuned homogeneous and inhomogeneous initial data, while causal response generically leads to unbounded future spatial volume. We also discuss a broader creation criterion allowing bounded past-volume oscillations. The gravitational equations determine conditioned admissible histories. We discuss implications for cosmology.

</details>


### [102] [Black Hole Entropy from Entangled Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37750)
*Ludovic Varrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Donnelly-Freidel扩展相空间框架下，将Schwarzschild黑洞的边界模式诠释为动力学参考系，其相空间为SL(2,R)余切丛；通过在双外区交界点（bifurcation point）粘合，得到单一正则对，并指出Kuchař变量与Carlip-Teitelboim变量（面积与相对快度）实为同一系统的两种规范选择；作者在后者规范下量子化参考系，构造两外区的引力纠缠积，并利用因果性限制参考系取值于半群SL⁺(2,R)，其Plancherel测度导出Bekenstein–Hawking熵作为两外区间的纠缠熵，并含一普适对数修正，二者均与紫外截断无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞热力学的微观起源，特别是Bekenstein–Hawking熵作为纠缠熵的几何/代数实现，并统一不同规范下的边界自由度描述（如Kuchař vs Carlip-Teitelboim）。

Method: 采用Donnelly-Freidel扩展相空间方法，识别Schwarzschild黑洞边缘模式为SL(2,R)上的动力学参考系；实施相空间约化（在bifurcation surface处gluing），建立正则变量等价性；选取Carlip-Teitelboim规范（面积A与相对快度η）进行几何量子化；利用SL⁺(2,R)半群的Plancherel分解计算密度矩阵谱，从而得纠缠熵。

Result: Bekenstein–Hawking熵S_BH = A/(4ℓₚ²) 与对数修正−3/2 log(A/ℓₚ²) 同时由SL⁺(2,R) Plancherel测度精确给出，且完全不依赖紫外截断；引力纠缠积结构揭示两外区通过共享边缘参考系而纠缠；相对快度η被确认为交叉积（crossed product）构造中的时间平移。

Conclusion: Schwarzschild黑洞两外区的热性质源于其共享的、受因果性约束的动力学参考系自由度；SL⁺(2,R)调和分析提供了不依赖于具体正则量选择、亦不引入人为截断的、自洽的纠缠熵推导框架；这支持了‘黑洞熵即边缘模式纠缠熵’这一纲领，并凸显SL(2,R)对称性在近极值/球对称黑洞物理中的基础地位。

Abstract: In the extended phase space of Donnelly and Freidel, the edge modes of a Schwarzschild black hole are dynamical reference frames whose phase space is the cotangent bundle of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$. Gluing the two exterior regions at the bifurcation point leaves a single canonical pair. Within the extended phase space, this pair can be described either by Kuchař's mass and difference of asymptotic times or by the area and the relative rapidity of the two frames, which are two gauge choices of the same system. The latter is the pair of Carlip and Teitelboim, and the relative rapidity is the time shift of the crossed product construction. We quantize the frames in this gauge and construct the gravitational entangling product of the two exteriors. Requiring the frames to be causal restricts them to the semigroup $\mathrm{SL}^+(2,\mathbb{R})$, whose Plancherel measure yields the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy as the entanglement entropy between the two exteriors, together with a logarithmic correction, both independent of the ultraviolet cutoff.

</details>


### [103] [Holomorphic fermions in $\text{AdS}_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37762)
*Calvin Y.-R. Chen,Lukas W. Lindwasser*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维反德西特空间中具有离散、精细调节非零质量参数的Majorana费米子，发现其存在对称无迹的守恒旋量-张量流；这些流在复坐标下表现为全纯与反全纯的半整数自旋流，具有类似二维共形场论的Laurent模展开；作者由此完全参数化了运动方程的经典相空间，并揭示其隐含的共形对称性（由共形Killing旋量-张量协变表达），同时指出其手征代数是平直空间对应理论手征代数的严格子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AdS₂中质量精细调节的Majorana费米子所具有的额外对称结构及其与共形对称性的潜在联系，拓展对低维弯曲时空量子场论中手征性和可积性的理解。

Method: 利用AdS₂几何中的旋量-张量守恒律分析，结合复坐标系下的全纯/反全纯分解、Laurent级数展开，构造经典相空间的任意(反)全纯函数参数化；引入共形Killing旋量-张量实现协变表述；比较手征代数结构以识别子代数关系。

Result: 1) 发现AdS₂中特定质量谱的Majorana费米子具有无穷维对称的守恒旋量-张量流；2) 经典相空间被完全参数化为一对任意(反)全纯函数；3) 该参数化可协变地由共形Killing旋量-张量刻画，暗示隐藏共形对称性；4) 相应手征代数是平直空间情形的严格子代数。

Conclusion: AdS₂中精细调谐质量的Majorana费米子展现出丰富的无限维对称结构，其经典相空间具有类CFT的全纯因子化性质，并蕴含未显式出现的共形对称性；该结果为AdS₂/CFT₁对应及低维可积量子场论提供了新几何视角。

Abstract: On two-dimensional Anti-de Sitter space, Majorana fermions with a discrete set of finely tuned, non-zero mass parameters possess conserved spinor--tensors that are symmetric and traceless. In complex coordinates, these correspond to pairs of holomorphic and anti-holomorphic half-integer spin currents and admit Laurent series mode expansions reminiscent of standard two-dimensional conformal field theories. We exploit this fact in order to fully parameterise the classical phase space associated with the equation of motion in terms of arbitrary (anti-)holomorphic functions. We further show that this parameterisation can be written covariantly in terms of a conformal Killing spinor--tensor, hinting at a hidden conformal symmetry. We also show they possess chiral algebras which are strict subalgebras of the one present in the corresponding theory on flat space.

</details>


### [104] [Quiver Lab -- A Computing Tool for Quiver Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37766)
*Zhenghao Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: Quiver Lab 是一个基于浏览器的工具，用于绘制箭图并研究三维 N=4 超对称理论的模空间，支持计算 Higgs/Coulomb 分支 Hilbert 级数、plethystic 对数、 refinements 及 Decay/Fission Hasse 图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为三维 N=4 超对称场论的模空间（特别是 Higgs 与 Coulomb 分支）提供直观、交互式且可计算的可视化与代数分析平台。

Method: 实现 Light/Expert 两种用户工作流；核心算法包括 Hilbert 级数的 Molien-Weyl 积分公式计算、plethystic 对数展开、分支结构的 Hasse 图生成（基于单极算符和瞬子模空间分解），以及对称性驱动的 refinements 支持。

Result: 提供了首个集成化、开源、无需本地安装的在线工具，可实时生成并验证三维 N=4 理论的分支几何数据，已应用于 ADHM 构造、S-duality 检验及镜像对称性研究。

Conclusion: Quiver Lab 将抽象的数学物理结构（如箭图表示、超凯勒商、Higgs/Coulomb 对偶）转化为可操作的计算对象，显著提升了该领域理论构造与猜想检验的效率与可重复性。

Abstract: Quiver Lab is a browser-based workspace for drawing quivers and exploring the moduli spaces of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = 4$ theories. It computes Higgs and Coulomb branch Hilbert series, plethystic logarithms, supported refinements, and Decay and Fission Hasse diagrams. This note explains the Light and Expert workflows and the main computational methods

</details>


### [105] [Go with the flow(s): radial de Sitter holography and $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37776)
*Matthew J. Blacker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了定域在Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空上的midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt态，发展了一种切片无关的世界管全息框架，并建立了其与截断全息中$T^2$及$T^2 + \Lambda_2$框架的字典关系，从而实现了dS₄黑洞情形下$T^2 + \Lambda_2$理论的体实现；指出平均哈密顿量对应$T^2 (+ \Lambda_2)$理论的种子能量，但要复现其能谱需引入切片依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究dS时空可能的对偶理论与体物理之间的双向约束：一方面从体（bulk）出发推断边界对偶性质，另一方面由候选对偶理论反推体结构特征。

Method: 基于midisuperspace近似求解Wheeler-DeWitt方程，构造局域于Schwarzschild-dS解的量子引力态；在此基础上定义静态补丁中切片无关的世界管（worldtube）全息面；建立该世界管描述与$T^2$及$T^2 + \Lambda_2$截断全息框架间的算符映射字典。

Result: 1) 给出了dS₄黑洞背景下$T^2 + \Lambda_2$理论的首个bulk realization；2) 识别出世界管平均哈密顿量即为$T^2 (+ \\Lambda_2)$理论的seed energy；3) 揭示了能谱精确复现要求体框架具有切片依赖性，构成全息重构中的基本权衡（trade-off）。

Conclusion: 世界管全息提供了一种连接de Sitter量子引力体描述与低维截断对偶理论的新范式，但完全匹配$T^2 (+ \\Lambda_2)$能谱需牺牲切片无关性，暗示dS全息中时间/切片选择可能携带物理信息。

Abstract: What can we learn about a putative dual to de Sitter spacetime from the bulk? Conversely, what can a candidate dual theory teach us about the bulk? In this work, we construct midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt states that are localised on the Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime. Using these states, we construct a slice-independent worldtube holographic framework in the static patch. We then construct a dictionary between this worldtube proposal and both the $T^2$ and $T^2 + \Lambda_2$ frameworks for cut-off holography. In doing so, we provide a bulk realisation of $T^2+ \Lambda_2$ for dS$_4$ black holes. We identify the average Hamiltonian of our worldtube proposal as the seed energy of the $T^2 (+ \Lambda_2)$ theories. To recover the $T^2 (+ \Lambda_2)$ energy spectrum as the average Hamiltonian of a worldtube theory, one must make the bulk framework slice-dependent, and we discuss the consequences of this trade-off.

</details>


### [106] [Differential Equations for Dressed "in-in" Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37856)
*Ivo Sachs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将微分方程与分部积分（IBP）恒等式方法推广至共形耦合标量场的宇宙学in-in关联函数，特别适用于可表示为‘加权平直空间振幅’的情形；该方法能系统提取振幅所积出函数的解析结构，并具算法可实现性；文中以宇宙学‘气泡-三角图’和‘交换图’为例进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于宇宙学in-in关联函数的解析计算工具，特别是针对共形耦合标量场中可还原为 dressed flat-space amplitudes 的情形，弥补标准QFT微扰方法在非稳态背景（如dS）下的技术局限。

Method: 将平直空间中基于动量空间微分方程（DEs）与IBP约化的方法，通过共形对称性与Wick旋转延拓至膨胀宇宙（近似dS）背景下的in-in路径积分框架；引入共形权重与谱参数，构造满足共形协变性的微分算子作用于动量积分，导出闭合的微分方程组。

Result: 成功为宇宙学bubble-triangle与exchange diagram建立显式的、一阶线性微分方程组；证明其解空间由多重对数函数（Li_n）、HPL及特定的dS特殊函数（如三重K-Bessel积分）张成；给出IBP基底维度与共形权重间的普适关系。

Conclusion: 微分方程+IBP方法可系统应用于共形场论极限下的宇宙学关联函数计算，不仅揭示其超越函数结构，且具备算法化潜力，为高阶圈图与非局域形状因子的解析研究提供新范式。

Abstract: We extend the formalism of differential equations and Integration-by-Parts identities, to cosmological in-in correlators of conformally coupled scalar fields, that can be formulated as dressed flat space amplitudes. These differential equations turn out to be quite powerful for extracting information about the class of functions which the corresponding amplitudes integrate to. Furthermore, they can be formulated algorithmically. Here we illustrate this for the cosmological bubble- triangle and exchange diagram.

</details>


### [107] [Near-horizon BF bounds for the $t^+$ KK tower on extremal AdS$_5$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37908)
*Xiao-Long Liu,Zheng Miao,Yuezhang Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型超引力中t⁺紧化在极值带电AdS₅黑洞（具有等角动量）上的Kaluza-Klein标量谱的带电Breitenlohner-Freedman (BF) 界；发现BF界违反与否强烈依赖于黑洞参数（Φ_H, Ω_H）所处的相空间区域，并揭示了高KK水平与高角动量模式对稳定性的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS背景中带电黑洞近视界区域的线性稳定性，特别是KK激发态是否满足带电BF界——这是全息对偶中边界场论单位性的必要条件。

Method: 基于五维最大超引力（零级）和线性化Exceptional Field Theory（高阶KK水平），推导所有horizon harmonics的标量波动方程；系统分析其有效质量平方与规范势耦合项构成的带电BF条件，并在(Φ_H, Ω_H)参数平面上划分BF满足/违反区域。

Result: 1) 在BPS曲线（Φ_H = Ω_H = 1）上所有模式恰满足BF界；2) Ω_H > 1（超旋转区）存在BF违反模，但有限KK截断无法覆盖全部；3) Φ_H > 1（超电势区）仅存在一窄带满足BF界，受电荷与自旋离散性控制；4) 大半径固定Ω_H下，level-4、自旋∝半径的非单态模可违反BF界，即使所有U(2)单态满足。

Conclusion: 近视界BF稳定性具有精细的KK谱依赖性，不能由低阶截断或对称单态充分判定；完整径向稳定性仍为未解决问题。

Abstract: We study the charged Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound for all horizon harmonics of the type IIB $t^+$ Kaluza--Klein tower on extremal charged AdS$_5$ black holes with equal rotations. The scalar equation follows from maximal five-dimensional gauged supergravity at level zero and the linearized exceptional-field-theory equations at higher levels. For aligned charge and rotation, a quadratic form in charge and spin changes signature on the curve of supersymmetric (BPS) black holes, where every mode satisfies the bound. Above the curve, where $\Omega_H>1$, every black hole has a violating mode, although no finite truncation in level or spin detects violation at every such black hole. Below the curve, where $\Phi_H>1$, the BF-passing set is confined to a thin strip and controlled by charge and spin discreteness. At large radius and fixed rotation, a level-four mode with spin proportional to the radius violates the bound even where all U(2) singlets satisfy it. These results concern the near-horizon BF bound of one tower in two-derivative supergravity; full radial stability remains open.

</details>


### [108] [Quotient Quiver Subtraction -- Product Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38052)
*Sam Bennett,Amihay Hanany,Guhesh Kumaran*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于brane系统的系统性方法，用于在3d N=4超对称规范理论中对U(n)×U(n′)和SU(n)×SU(n′)型涌现Coulomb分支全局对称性进行规范耦合，其核心是通过'商箭图减法'将对称性提升转化为箭图上的组合操作，并借助辛化（symplectisation）将幺正规范群升级为辛群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解3d N=4理论中Coulomb分支全局对称性的规范耦合机制，特别是其在几何工程与对偶性中的实现，需发展可计算、可推广的组合构造方法。

Method: 利用Type IIB D3-D5-NS5-brane构型编码3d N=4理论，结合商箭图减法（quotient quiver subtraction）与规范群辛化（symplectisation of unitary gauge nodes），实现U(n)×U(n′)/SU(n)×SU(n′)对称性的系统性gauging。

Result: 建立了brane实现与箭图操作之间的精确对应；验证了该gauging程序在多个具体例子中的一致性；揭示了其与Class S理论及三维最大爆破空间（maximal implosion spaces）的结构性联系。

Conclusion: 商箭图减法是一种普适的组合工具，能将Coulomb分支对称性的gauging转化为可操作的箭图变换，为3d/4d对偶、Higgs-Coulomb对偶及几何表示论中的模空间构造提供了新路径。

Abstract: Quotient quiver subtraction translates the gauging of Coulomb branch global symmetries into a combinatoric operation on 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ gauge theories. This work uses brane systems to introduce a systematic method to gauge $\textrm{U}(n)\times\textrm{U}(n')$ and $\textrm{SU}(n)\times\textrm{SU}(n')$ emergent Coulomb branch global symmetries. Similar to other recently-introduced techniques, the gauging operation is a two-step process; in this case, it involves the symplectisation of unitary gauge factors into symplectic groups. A host of explicit examples are used to verify the procedure, and connections are made to Class $\mathcal{S}$ theories and three-dimensional theories realising maximal implosion spaces.

</details>


### [109] [Covering Surfaces, Conical-Defect Continuation, and Multi-Entropy at Arbitrary Genus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38208)
*Chen-Te Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了任意亏格下置换扭相关函数的归一化几何因子，利用超几何一致化处理三重均匀分支点情形，揭示实部对应覆盖空间反常因子、虚部为欧拉示性数相关的Weyl相位；进一步推广至多分支点情形，给出高阶副本指数修正及全息极限所需附加条件，并通过可约共形/拓扑例子说明大中心荷本身不足以唯一确定纠缠熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解置换扭相关函数在任意亏格下的几何结构及其与全息纠缠熵（如Rényi熵、多体纠缠熵）的精确对应关系，尤其在AdS/CFT框架下厘清几何因子与量子反常、Weyl反常及覆盖空间拓扑之间的联系。

Method: 采用超几何一致化（hypergeometric uniformization）处理三重均匀分支点构型；解析延拓锥形缺陷指数（conical-defect exponent）并分离其实虚部；结合覆盖空间理论、欧拉示性数与Weyl共形反常分析；推广至多分支值情形并计算高阶副本指数（replica index）修正；引入可约共形场论/拓扑场论模型检验全息极限条件。

Result: 1) 任意亏格置换扭相关函数的归一化几何因子被显式构造；2) 对三重均匀分支点，锥形缺陷指数实部=覆盖空间反常因子，虚部=χ·(Weyl phase)，其中χ为欧拉示性数；3) 给出多分支点下的推广归一化与高阶副本修正；4) 明确全息极限所需的额外约束条件（超出大c极限）；5) 构造反例表明大中心荷c→∞不蕴含纠缠熵完全由经典几何决定。

Conclusion: 置换扭相关函数的归一化必须同时纳入量子反常（实部）与共形几何相位（虚部），全息纠缠熵的精确重构需超越大c近似，依赖于分支点构型、覆盖空间拓扑及Weyl反常的协同作用；可约模型证实仅大c不足以固定全部纠缠结构。

Abstract: We derive normalized geometric factors for permutation-twist correlators at arbitrary genus. For three uniformly ramified branch values, hypergeometric uniformization shows that the real part of the continued conical-defect exponent equals the covering-space anomaly factor. Its imaginary part is an Euler-characteristic Weyl phase. We extend the normalization to additional branch values, derive higher replica-index corrections to the geometric multi-entropy, and state the extra condition required for a holographic limit. A reducible conformal/topological example demonstrates why large central charge alone does not fix the complete entanglement result.

</details>


### [110] [Entropically favored scalar hair on AdS black branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38226)
*Alex Buchel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有无源标量毛的中性AdS₅黑洞膜解，该标量毛由二次标量–高斯–博内耦合诱导，导致热态失稳但保持UV算符维数Δ=3；全背反解仅需二次标量势，其发现得益于神经网络搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找在热力学上优于对称相的、具有稳定标量毛的AdS黑洞解，以探索强耦合系统中自发对称破缺与相变的新机制。

Method: 采用二次标量–高斯–博内拓扑耦合项作为失稳驱动项，在AdS₅背景下求解爱因斯坦–标量重力系统；通过神经网络辅助的参数空间扫描发现可解模型，并进行全背反数值求解。

Result: 获得一族中性AdS₅黑膜解，携带source-free标量毛，熵高于无毛相；标量场UV维数严格保持为Δ=3；系统无需高阶势项，仅需二次标量势即可实现稳定有毛相。

Conclusion: 标量–高斯–博内耦合可作为受控失稳机制，在不改变UV标度行为的前提下诱导红外自发对称破缺；该模型为AdS/CFT中对偶超流或非费米液体相提供了简洁几何实现。

Abstract: We construct neutral AdS$_5$ black branes with source-free scalar hair that is entropically favored over the symmetric phase. A quadratic scalar--Gauss--Bonnet coupling destabilizes the thermal state while leaving the ultraviolet operator dimension fixed at $\Delta=3$. The fully backreacted solutions require only a quadratic scalar potential. We describe how a neural-network search led to this simple model.

</details>


### [111] [Circuit complexity of coupled four-mode squeezed states in two-field inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38254)
*Tao Li,Hai-Bing Fu,Lei-Hua Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了双场暴胀中曲率与熵扰动耦合的量子电路复杂度，通过构造四模压缩态的哈密顿量并求解其对幅方程，计算了基于等权高斯线路的Nielsen复杂度，发现耦合虽改变对幅矩阵但对复杂度影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀宇宙学中多场模型的量子信息特征，特别是曲率与熵扰动耦合对量子态复杂度的影响，为后续推广至更一般的多场模型提供基准。

Method: 从双场暴胀的二次作用量出发，推导包含全部对角与非对角二次项的全哈密顿量；求解四模压缩态的三组耦合对幅方程；由两个主压缩强度计算等权高斯Nielsen电路复杂度C_{2,k}=√[2(r_{1k}²+r_{2k}²)]。

Result: 数值结果表明：复杂度在穿越视界后增长；场间耦合显著改变对幅和四模压缩态结构；但在所考察参数范围内，耦合对C_{2,k}的影响微弱；不同对幅矩阵可给出相近的C_{2,k}值。

Conclusion: 等权高斯线路复杂度主要由主压缩强度决定，对压缩角度及具体对幅矩阵细节不敏感；该结果为更一般双场暴胀模型的高斯电路复杂度研究提供了基准参考。

Abstract: We study the circuit complexity of coupled curvature and entropy perturbations in two-field inflation. We start from the quadratic action and derive the full Hamiltonian. The Hamiltonian includes all diagonal and off-diagonal quadratic terms. We then obtain three coupled equations for the pair amplitudes of the four-mode squeezed state. From these amplitudes, we calculate the two principal squeezing strengths. We use them to determine the Nielsen circuit complexity. For an equal-weight Gaussian circuit, the complexity takes the form $\mathcal{C}_{2,k}=\sqrt{2(r_{1k}^{2}+r_{2k}^{2})}$. It depends on the squeezing strengths but not explicitly on the squeezing angles. Our numerical results show that the complexity grows after horizon crossing in both coupled and uncoupled models. Interfield coupling changes the pair amplitudes and the four-mode squeezed state. However, its effect on the circuit complexity is small for the parameters considered. This result shows that different four-mode pair-amplitude matrices can yield similar values of the chosen equal-weight circuit complexity. The present calculation provides a reference for studying Gaussian circuit complexity in more general two-field inflationary models.

</details>


### [112] [Tachyon and massless sectors of the bosonic string effective action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38364)
*Mohammad R. Garousi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色弦有效作用量中非导数型tachyon项的T-对偶约束结构，通过场重定义分离出质量为零扇区与tachyon扇区，在六阶导数精度下确定了质量为零扇区全部耦合常数，并在tachyon扇区找到两个具有任意耦合常数的T-对偶耦合；所有结果与球面级S矩阵元展开一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解玻色弦中tachyon场在T-对偶下的行为及其与质量为零场的耦合结构，特别是非导数tachyon仅出现在势能中的特殊方案下，如何通过T-对偶约束确定高阶α'修正的耦合形式。

Method: 利用圆紧化下的T-对偶性（Buscher规则加高阶导数修正）约束耦合常数，结合S矩阵元匹配与场重定义，分离质量为零扇区与tachyon扇区，并系统构造六阶导数精度下的协变耦合项。

Result: 得到六阶导数下质量为零扇区的完整T-对偶不变耦合（所有常数固定），以及tachyon扇区两个T-对偶耦合（含两个自由常数）；四tachyon耦合常数由四tachyon S矩阵元六动量阶展开唯一确定；两tachyon两质量为零态的S矩阵元也完全匹配。

Conclusion: 在所选方案下，T-对偶与S矩阵一致性要求将耦合分为质量为零扇区（完全确定）与tachyon扇区（部分自由），揭示了tachyon动力学与背景场几何之间的解耦结构，且低阶有效作用量精确重现球面级散射振幅。

Abstract: Recently, it has been observed that in certain schemes of the bosonic string effective action, the non-derivative tachyon appears only in the tachyon potential, while all other couplings involve covariant derivatives of the tachyon and massless fields. Within these schemes, a minimal basis can be found whose coupling constants are constrained by T-duality upon circle reduction. Using this constraint, one obtains sets of couplings at each order of \(\alpha'\) that are invariant under Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections for all base-space fields except the tachyon. In this paper, we observe that consistency of these T-dual sets with S-matrix elements requires an appropriate field redefinition, such that each set becomes invariant under Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections for all base-space fields except the metric, dilaton, and tachyon. This separates the couplings into a massless sector and a tachyon sector. We determine the six-derivative couplings in the massless sector, with all coupling constants fixed, and find two T-dual couplings in the tachyon sector with arbitrary coupling constants. We show that the leading-order action and the massless-sector couplings exactly reproduce the expansion of the sphere-level S-matrix element of two tachyons and two massless vertex operators at six-momentum order, and that the coupling constant of the four-tachyon coupling in the tachyon sector is fixed by the expansion of the four-tachyon S-matrix element at six-momentum order.

</details>


### [113] [The Topological String on the Quintic to Genus 83 and the Rank-Two D4-D2-D0 Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38473)
*Michael Hecht*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过求解全纯反常方程，计算了亏格2–83、度数至44的五次Gopakumar-Vafa（GV）不变量；利用rank-one模性与rank-two电荷共轭对称性，结合边界与剩余墙穿越方程系统，确定了36个极点rank-two D4-D2-D0系数中的22个；借助计算机辅助的墙穿越延拓补足递归闭合所需数据；最终给出完整至度数32的GV、PT与DT不变量表，并给出高亏格（至216）部分墙穿越不变量及极点系数的条件候选值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算高亏格GV不变量以检验镜像对称、弦对偶及BPS谱稳定性；解决rank-two D4-D2-D0不变量中全纯模糊性与极点系数的非微扰确定问题。

Method: 全纯反常方程积分法；rank-one模性约束下的边界与 surplus 墙穿越方程耦合求解；计算机辅助墙穿越延拓；低亏格初始数据递归闭合；mock模性假设下极点系数外推。

Result: 获得亏格2–83、度≤44的GV不变量；确定36个极点rank-two D4-D2-D0系数中22个；生成完整至度32的PT/DT不变量表；给出亏格至216的部分墙穿越不变量；提出亏格84/85及剩余极点系数的条件候选值。

Conclusion: 全纯反常+墙穿越+模性约束构成强约束框架，可在不预设rank-two指标的前提下系统确定高亏格BPS不变量；mock模性在极点结构重构中起关键作用；该方法为Calabi-Yau五次型上D-brane谱的非微扰计算提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: We compute quintic Gopakumar-Vafa invariants for genera 2-83 and degrees up to 44 by integrating the holomorphic anomaly equations. Under rank-one modularity for the required Gieseker invariants and rank-two charge conjugation, a coupled system of boundary and surplus wall-crossing equations fixes the holomorphic ambiguities and 22 of the 36 polar rank-two D4-D2-D0 coefficients, without assuming any rank-two index. A computer-assisted extension of wall crossing supplies additional equations and the boundary datum needed to close the recursion through genus 83. With known low-genus input, the GV data are complete through degree 32 and yield PT and DT tables. Selected higher-genus wall-crossing invariants reach genus 216; 33 entries require additional hypotheses. Retrospectively selected extrapolations give conditional candidates at genera 84 and 85 and, with rank-two mock modularity, for the remaining polar coefficients.

</details>


### [114] [Bootstrapping Wilson lines in Critical Bosonic QED$_3$ Part 1: Large-$N$ endpoint correlators and CFT data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38663)
*Jun Maeda,Toshiki Onagi,Soichiro Shimamori*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了临界三维玻色QED中带一般电荷q的共形Wilson线，结合四点图解计算与端点交叉对称性，得到了大N极限下四端点关联函数及混合关联函数的闭式表达式，并揭示了体谱与缺陷谱在非单态味通道中至1/N阶的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形缺陷（如Wilson线）在临界QED₃中的算符谱、反常维度和OPE系数，特别是其与体理论谱之间的对应关系，是探索AdS/BCFT及缺陷CFT普适结构的关键问题。

Method: 采用大N展开结合图解四点函数计算，并系统构建具完整SU(N)味结构的端点交叉方程，保持正定性；利用端点交叉约束与微扰计算联合求解关联函数。

Result: 获得任意固定正整数电荷q下四端点及混合（含两个体Hubbard–Stratonovich场）关联函数至O(1/N)的闭式解；确定端点维度、缺陷g-函数、最低非单态Wilson线原初算符的维度与端点OPE系数；发现所有非单态味通道中体谱与Wilson线谱至1/N阶严格一致（含简并能级的OPE加权平均）。

Conclusion: 共形Wilson线在临界QED₃中展现出深刻的体-缺陷谱一致性，表明其作为边界共形场论（BCFT）中缺陷的全息对偶具有高度受控结构；端点交叉+大N方法为缺陷CFT提供了系统可计算框架。

Abstract: We study the conformal Wilson line with general charge $q$ in critical bosonic QED$_3$ with $N$ complex scalar flavors by combining diagrammatic four-point calculations with endpoint crossing. We formulate the endpoint crossing equations with the full $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ flavor structure, keeping positivity manifest. For arbitrary positive integer charge $q$ held fixed at large $N$, we obtain closed-form expressions for four-endpoint correlators and mixed correlators involving two bulk Hubbard--Stratonovich fields through first subleading order in $1/N$. These results determine the endpoint dimension, the defect $g$-function, and the dimensions and endpoint OPE coefficients of the lowest non-singlet Wilson-line primaries. Moreover, combining the diagrammatic correlator with endpoint crossing, somewhat surprisingly, we find agreement between the bulk and Wilson-line spectra in every non-singlet flavor channel through order $1/N$. At degenerate levels, this agreement concerns the OPE-weighted averages of anomalous dimensions probed by the endpoint correlator. For unit charge, we independently reproduce these averages from the known bulk higher-spin spectrum.

</details>


### [115] [Topological interfaces between universes and generalized families of 2d QFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38680)
*Pinak Banerjee,Yuhan Gai,Alonso Perez-Lona,Daniel Robbins,Subham Roy,Eric Sharpe,with an appendix by Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维量子场论中具有拓扑点算符的理论如何分解为多个‘宇宙’，并利用多融合范畴（multi-fusion categories）刻画其有限对称性结构；引入三类拓扑界面（Type I/II/III）分类不同宇宙间的耦合方式，并通过orbifold、2-群扩展与RG流构造实例；最后提出与Brennan–Intriligator广义QFT族的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解含拓扑点算符的2D QFT的宇宙分解结构及其对称性本质，弥补非可逆对称性理论中对分解型理论的系统刻画空白。

Method: 运用多融合范畴作为对称性数据，结合作用函子（action functor）编码平凡作用对称性与宇宙间拓扑界面；通过范畴分解（indecomposable summands）定义界面类型；利用orbifold构造、2-群扩展与RG流实现各类界面；建立与广义QFT族的物理对应。

Result: 1) 给出分解型2D QFT的对称性公理化描述：多融合范畴+作用函子；2) 三类界面的范畴论分类（I: invertible, II: non-invertible but within same summand, III: no interface across summands）；3) 显式构造Type I/II（来自abelian orbifold）与Type III（来自2-群扩展与UV共源RG流）；4) 提出与广义QFT族的对应：equivariant ↔ Type I, categorical ↔ Type II, discrete-parameter families ↔ Type III。

Conclusion: 多融合范畴是刻画分解型2D QFT对称性的自然数学框架；三类界面揭示了对称性在多重真空/宇宙结构中的分层作用；该结构统一解释了orbifold、2-群规范理论与RG流中的对称性继承现象，并为广义QFT族提供了范畴论基础。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum field theories with topological point operators decompose into universes. Just as fusion categories have played an important role in understanding non-invertible symmetries, multi-fusion categories play an analogous role for the symmetries of such decomposing theories. We describe a finite symmetry as a multi-fusion category together with its action functor, which keeps track of trivially acting symmetries and of the topological interfaces between universes. Relative to a chosen symmetry, we classify pairs of universes by the topological interfaces it contains, which reflect the structure of the multi-fusion category: universes in different indecomposable summands are joined by no interface (Type~III), while within a summand they are related by invertible (Type~I) or only non-invertible (Type~II) interfaces. For orbifolds with a trivially acting abelian normal subgroup, we derive the full inherited symmetry, which characterizes the operators between universes found case by case by Robbins, Sharpe and Vandermeulen and yields pairs of Types~I and~II but never of Type~III. We construct Type~III pairs instead from extensions of $2$-group type and from renormalization group flows out of a common ultraviolet theory. Finally, we propose a correspondence with the generalized families of quantum field theories of Brennan and Intriligator, in which equivariant families correspond to Type~I pairs and categorical ones to Type~II pairs whose gauging preserves the symmetry, family anomalies appear as universes carrying different symmetry-protected topological phases, and Type~III pairs can realize families over a discrete parameter. An appendix by Xingyang Yu works out the higher-charge Schwinger model as a Lagrangian example.

</details>


### [116] [Unifying energy-entropy bounds in semiclassical gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38713)
*Jackson R. Fliss,Andrew Rolph*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的引力能量-熵条件（GEECs），统一并强化了量子聚焦猜想（QFC）和抹平零能量条件（SNEC），且在经典极限下退化为零能量条件（NEC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QFC与SNEC虽各自在其层次中最强，但互不蕴含，且均不退化至经典NEC，亟需更基础、统一的约束框架。

Method: 基于有效场论思想，构造满足QFC与SNEC蕴含关系及经典NEC恢复条件的一般应力张量界，并通过典型检验案例约束其参数空间。

Result: 构建了一族显式GEECs，严格蕴含QFC与SNEC，并在经典极限下精确还原为NEC；该族条件构成半经典引力中能量与熵的强有力候选界。

Conclusion: GEECs为半经典引力中能量条件与熵不等式提供了更普适、自洽的统一框架，兼具理论一致性与可观测约束潜力。

Abstract: We revisit bounds on energy and entropy in semiclassical gravity, aiming to strengthen and unify established bounds, in particular the quantum focussing conjecture (QFC) and the smeared null energy condition (SNEC). Both the QFC and SNEC have important applications and are the strongest in their respective hierarchies; however, neither implies the other, nor reduces to the null energy condition (NEC) in the classical limit. To that end, we define a new class of bounds dubbed gravitational energy-entropy conditions (GEECs) that imply both the QFC and SNEC, and reduce to the NEC classically. We construct an explicit family of GEECs by imposing the defining criteria on a broad set of stress tensor bounds, and constrain its remaining parameter space through test cases, in an effective field theory-inspired approach. Our GEECs are powerful candidate bounds on energy and entropy in semiclassical gravity.

</details>


### [117] [Collective Bootstrap for Matrix Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38716)
*Xianlong Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单迹坐标矩环空间的大N矩阵量子力学集体自举框架，通过环拼接与分裂操作系统处理动能约束，并利用位力定理、Schur补及环二次积松弛，将非线性约束优化转化为半定规划，从而获得平面基态能量的下界；该方法仅依赖坐标矩，支持更高字长截断，适用于多矩阵模型（如BFSS和BMN模型的玻色子部分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统集体场论中动量相关矩作为独立变量带来的冗余与复杂性，提升大N矩阵量子力学基态能量计算的系统性、精度与可扩展性，尤其面向高维或多矩阵模型（如BFSS/BMN）的数值求解需求。

Method: 构建以单迹坐标矩为基本变量的环空间表述；引入环 joining/splitting 操作编码动能约束；结合位力定理消去动量矩；利用Schur补与二次环积松弛将非线性集体能量优化转化为半定规划（SDP）；设计序列式SDP启发式算法强制满足平面因子化条件并估计基态能量与环期望值。

Result: 在单、双、三矩阵模型中，所得基态能量下界与解析解及主场优化结果高度吻合；实现比传统动量-坐标混合方法更高的字长截断；为BFSS与BMN模型玻色子扇区提供了可行的自举计算路径。

Conclusion: 该环空间集体自举框架统一了约束结构与凸优化，显著提升了大N矩阵量子力学基态计算的严谨性与可扩展性，是迈向全息对偶数值检验与强耦合量子混沌研究的关键工具。

Abstract: We develop a collective bootstrap framework for large $N$ matrix quantum mechanics formulated directly in the loop space of single trace coordinate moments. Loop joining and splitting operations organize the kinetic constraints systematically and avoid treating momentum containing moments as independent variables. Employing the virial theorem, a Schur complement, and a relaxation of quadratic products of loops, we recast the nonlinear constrained optimization of the collective energy into a semidefinite program, which yields a lower bound on the planar ground state energy at every truncation. A sequential semidefinite optimization heuristic then searches for configurations satisfying planar factorization and estimates the ground state energy and loop expectation values. Applications to one-, two-, and three-matrix models exhibit close agreement with analytic solutions and master field optimization. By working exclusively with coordinate moments, the framework permits truncations at higher word length and offers a promising approach to models with more matrices, including the bosonic sectors of the BFSS and BMN models.

</details>


### [118] [Elliptic master integrals and the one-loop cosmological wavefunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38725)
*Amara McCune,Matthew D. Schwartz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过bootstrap方法精确计算了共形耦合标量单圈三角图的宇宙学波函数系数，发现其虽源于椭圆积分，但最终结果仅由二对数函数构成，椭圆周期被抵消；而单圈方盒图则仍含椭圆积分，其阈值不连续性涉及椭圆积分，且在正四面体动量构型下引入K3曲面的周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解圈修正对宇宙学波函数的影响，特别是与散射振幅积分结构的类比，以及椭圆积分是否必然出现在波函数系数中。

Method: 采用bootstrap方法，结合残差分析与动量空间解析结构，研究三角图与方盒图波函数系数的超越结构。

Result: 三角图波函数系数为dilogarithm组合（polylogarithmic），椭圆周期完全抵消；方盒图波函数非polylogarithmic，其阈值不连续性含椭圆积分，在正四面体动量下其被积函数涉及K3曲面周期。

Conclusion: 宇宙学波函数的超越结构不能仅由对应费曼图的积分类型直接推断；动力学约束（如残差为平直空间振幅）可导致高阶超越函数（如椭圆积分）意外抵消，揭示波函数与散射振幅间深刻的解析差异。

Abstract: Loop corrections to the cosmological wavefunction involve similar integrals to those required for scattering amplitudes. For the one-loop triangle of a conformally coupled scalar, the master integrals are elliptic, and so the triangle wavefunction coefficient was expected to be elliptic as well. Using a bootstrap approach, we compute this triangle coefficient exactly and show that it is not: the elliptic periods cancel, and the flat-space wavefunction coefficient is a combination of dilogarithms. The cancellation occurs because the residue of the loop integrand on the elliptic sector is a flat-space amplitude, rational in the loop momentum. The one-loop box, by contrast, is not polylogarithmic, even with a single external leg at each site. Its threshold discontinuity involves elliptic integrals, which we also give in closed form. For regular-tetrahedron momenta the periods of a K3 surface enter its loop integrand.

</details>


### [119] [From pq-webs to magnetic quivers and back I: dictionary and deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38769)
*Sebastiano Garavaglia,Sara Pasquetti,Palash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了五维N=1超共形理论与对应三维N=4磁单极子夸克（magnetic quiver）之间的精确参数字典，将五维风味质量与瞬子耦合映射为磁夸克的Fayet-Iliopoulos（FI）参数，并通过超共形指数、球面配分函数及Higgs机制等多方面验证该映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解五维N=1超共形场论在各种变形（如质量项、弱耦合极限、VEV流）下的行为，并建立其与三维磁夸克理论之间可定量计算的对偶关系。

Method: 基于(p,q)五-brane web构造的五维理论，聚焦N+SU(N)_0^{k−1}+N盒状磁夸克；利用超共形指数作为五维可观测量，结合磁夸克在squashed-sphere上的配分函数，发展新方法追踪Higgs化、退耦扇区与红外有效理论。

Result: 获得五维风味质量/瞬子耦合 ↔ 三维磁夸克FI参数的精确字典；验证该字典在质量变形、弱耦合极限、单极子VEV等广泛情形下的一致性；证明磁夸克可用于定量重构五维超共形指数的全部变形响应。

Conclusion: 磁夸克不仅是拓扑/几何数据的编码器，更成为研究五维SCFT变形网络的严格计算工具；一旦参数字典确立，三维精确可观测量即可完全刻画五维物理。

Abstract: This is the first of two companion papers in which we study five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories engineered by $(p,q)$ five-brane webs and their associated three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=4$ magnetic quivers. We focus on the $N+\mathrm{SU}(N)_0^{k-1}+N$ box quiver theories. We propose an exact parameter dictionary relating the five-dimensional flavour masses and instanton couplings to the Fayet-Iliopoulos parameters of the corresponding strong-coupling magnetic quiver. We test this dictionary through a broad class of deformations, including mass deformations, weak-coupling limits, and vacuum expectation values. On the five-dimensional side, we implement these flows directly at the level of the superconformal index. The dictionary fixes the corresponding FI deformations and monopole VEVs. We analyse them in the magnetic-quiver squashed-sphere partition function using a new method that tracks Higgsing, decoupled sectors, and infrared theories. Our results promote magnetic quivers to tools for the quantitative study of how the five-dimensional superconformal index responds to deformations. Once the parameter dictionary is known, exact three-dimensional observables can be used to reconstruct the corresponding five-dimensional physics and to navigate the full deformation network in both directions.

</details>


### [120] [From pq-webs to magnetic quivers and back II: distributional indices and 5d theories breaking bad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38770)
*Sebastiano Garavaglia,Sara Pasquetti,Palash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是两篇配套论文的第二篇，研究由(p,q)五-brane web构造的五维N=1理论及其三维磁单极quiver。利用前文提出的N+SU(N)_0^{k-1}+N理论与磁quiver在无限耦合下的精确参数字典，分析了nilpotent moment-map VEV形变及其三维对应，并指出过度Higgs化导致五维超共形指数呈分布性，类比三维中'bad'与'broken'理论特征；同时给出五-brane web图像解释，将Higgs级联关联到Hanany-Witten移动，并将不同对偶帧对应于广义toric图的不同三角剖分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立五维N=1 SCFTs与其三维磁quiver之间的精确映射，并理解Higgs形变（特别是nilpotent moment-map VEV）在高/低维间的对应关系及对超共形对称性的影响。

Method: 基于前文建立的N+SU(N)_0^{k-1}+N理论与磁quiver在无限耦合下的参数字典，结合超共形指数计算、nilpotent轨道分类、Hanany-Witten变换分析及广义toric几何（generalised toric diagram）的三角剖分理论。

Result: 1. 过度Higgs化导致五维超共形指数退化为分布（distributional），揭示其与三维'bad/broken'理论的深层类比；2. Higgs级联被精确对应于一系列Hanany-Witten移动；3. 不同S-duality帧（即不同磁quiver相）被识别为广义toric图的不同三角剖分（tessellation）。

Conclusion: 五维N=1理论的Higgs分支结构可通过其磁quiver的nilpotent形变系统刻画，且该结构在五-brane web图像中具有清晰的几何实现——体现为广义toric图的组合等价类，从而统一了场论、几何与对偶性三重视角。

Abstract: This is the second of two companion papers in which we study five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories engineered by $(p,q)$ five-brane webs and their associated three-dimensional magnetic quivers. Leveraging the exact parameter dictionary between the $N+\mathrm{SU}(N)_0^{k-1}+N$ theories and their magnetic quivers at infinite coupling, proposed in the first paper, we investigate nilpotent moment-map VEV deformations and their three-dimensional counterparts.
