Result: Λc→πχ̄与Λc→Kχ̄分支比在物理区域内可达O(10^{-7})，不确定性包络在部分区域达10^{-6}；结果与ChPT及QCD因子化估算进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该类衰变是探测baryon-number-conserving but visibly-violating新物理的理想探针；STCF与Belle II有望覆盖部分预言区间，FCC-ee则具备探测更小分支比（<10^{-7}）的能力。

Abstract: Baryon-number-violating decays with missing energy probe new interactions connecting Standard-Model quarks to light invisible fermions. Such signatures can arise from dimension-six operators coupling three quarks to an SM-singlet fermion $\chi$, which escapes detection. With a suitable baryon-number assignment for $\chi$, total baryon number is conserved while its violation is apparent in the visible sector. We study the exclusive decays $\Lambda_c\to\pi\bar\chi$ and $\Lambda_c\to K\bar\chi$, determining the required form factors using light-cone sum rules with pion and kaon distribution amplitudes. The calculation includes leading-order two-particle contributions up to twist four and factorisable four-particle terms. Two $\Lambda_c$ interpolating currents are employed to assess the current dependence. Using single-operator benchmarks based on collider bounds, we obtain branching fractions of order $10^{-7}$ over sizeable parts of the physical region, with uncertainty envelopes reaching $10^{-6}$ in some scenarios. These are indicative estimates within the retained light-cone expansion. We compare with existing chiral-perturbation-theory and QCD-factorisation estimates and discuss experimental prospects. Future data from STCF and Belle II could probe part of the predicted range, while FCC-ee could reach smaller branching fractions.

</details>


### [163] [Vector- and tensor-meson resonance contributions to $χ_{cJ}\to PPV$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38178)
*Yuan-Guo Xu,Qin-Ze Song,Bo Lan,Ru-Min Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻矢量介子和张量介子共振态对χcJ→P1P2V三体衰变的贡献，采用准二体框架结合SU(3)味对称性分析VV→P1P2V和TP→P1P2V两类子过程，并分别考虑窄宽近似（NWA）与有限宽度效应（FWE），后者对运动学禁戒但通过共振高质尾部贡献的张量衰变尤为关键。预测共振带强度跨越多个数量级，ππρ、πK̄K∗、K̄Kϕ最强，而K̄Kω和η′KK∗则由张量带主导，构成探测T→PV张量共振的干净探针。结果为未来不变质量谱与振幅分析提供先验强度分布与通道优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解χcJ三体强子衰变中矢量/张量共振态的动力学贡献，特别是张量介子在运动学禁戒区域通过有限宽度效应的非微扰贡献；建立可检验的理论预言以指导实验分辨共振结构并提取T→PV耦合信息。

Method: 采用准二体分解框架，将χcJ→P1P2V分解为χcJ→VV→P1P2V（J=0,1,2）和χcJ→TP→P1P2V（J=1,2）两类路径；利用SU(3)味对称性约束二体顶点耦合；严格区分窄宽近似（NWA）与包含Breit–Wigner线形积分的有限宽度效应（FWE）处理，尤其对张量通道实施全运动学积分。

Result: 预测各末态中矢量带（VV）与张量带（TP）的相对强度及绝对大小：ππρ、πK̄K∗、K̄Kϕ共振带最强；K̄Kω与η′KK∗中张量带压倒性主导；给出各通道带内强度排序与数量级分布，作为振幅分析的先验输入。

Conclusion: 张量介子通过有限宽度效应在运动学禁戒区对χcJ→P1P2V有可观贡献；K̄Kω与η′KK∗是提取T→PV耦合的理想洁净通道；理论预测为BESIII/LHCb等实验开展不变质量谱拟合与联合振幅分析提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Light vector- and tensor-meson resonance contributions to the three-body decays $\chi_{cJ}\to P_1P_2V$ are studied in a quasi-two-body framework combined with SU(3) flavor relations for the two-body subprocesses, covering both the $\chi_{c0,1,2}\to VV\to P_1P_2V$ and $\chi_{c1,2}\to TP\to P_1P_2V$. Both the narrow-width approximation (NWA) and the finite-width effects (FWE) treatment are considered, the latter being essential for tensor subdecays that are kinematically closed at the nominal mass and contribute only through the high-mass tail of the resonance. The predicted resonance bands span several orders of magnitude, with the largest ones in the $\pi\pi\rho$, $\pi K\bar K^{*}$, and $K\bar K\phi$ final states, whereas the $K\bar K\omega$ and $\eta'KK^{*}$ channels are dominated by the tensor band and thus provide a clean window onto the $T\to PV$ tensor resonances. The band-by-band strengths and their ordering are intended as priors and channel priorities for future invariant-mass and amplitude analyses, and can be compared with the measured totals of several channels.

</details>


### [164] [Spectra and strong decays of open charm-bottom $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$ tetraquarks in an unequal-mass Born-Oppenheimer framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38217)
*Bowen Kang,Xi Xia,Tao Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Born-Oppenheimer近似下研究了S波开粲底四夸克态 $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$（$q=u,d,s$）的质量谱与强衰变行为，采用基于优化重轻轨道构建的有效势，处理非等质量重夸克源；计算了 $J^P = 0^+, 1^+, 2^+$ 下由两个颜色八重态耦合形成的颜色单态谱，得到系统性的味依赖质量位移与超精细劈裂，并估算了若干双体开味衰变道的偏宽度，为理论与实验探索提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展先前对隐含重味四夸克态的研究，处理开粲底系统中$c$与$b$夸克质量不等带来的动力学复杂性，建立更普适的计算框架以预测可观测的谱学与衰变特征。

Method: 采用Born-Oppenheimer近似，基于独立优化的重-轻轨道构造有效势；色结构限定为两个颜色八重态耦合成总颜色单态；量子数取$J^P = 0^+, 1^+, 2^+$；强衰变宽度通过色-自旋重耦合与唯象衰变模型估算。

Result: 获得$c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$四夸克态在$0^+, 1^+, 2^+$下的质量谱，呈现系统性味依赖位移（如$s$夸克导致质量升高）与由色磁相互作用主导的超精细劈裂；预测了如$c\bar{b}\to D^{(*)}B^{(*)}$类两体开味衰变的偏宽度，显示通道依赖的选择定则。

Conclusion: 该方法可稳健推广至非等质量重夸克四夸克体系；预测的质量谱与衰变模式为LHCb、Belle II等实验搜寻开粲底四夸克态提供了关键理论输入与鉴别依据。

Abstract: We investigate the mass spectra and strong decay channels of $S$-wave open charm-bottom $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$ ($q = u,d,s$) tetraquarks within the Born-Oppenheimer approximation. Extending our previous study of hidden heavy flavor tetraquarks, we account for the unequal-mass heavy quark sources and construct the effective potential based on independently optimized heavy-light orbitals. This makes the computational method more generally applicable. For the quantum numbers $J^P=0^+$, $1^+$, and $2^+$, we have obtained the spectra of the open charm-bottom $c\bar{b}q\bar{q}$ tetraquarks corresponding to color-singlet states constructed by coupling two color octets. The predicted masses exhibit systematic flavor-dependent shifts and characteristic hyperfine splittings. We also estimate partial widths for selected two-body open flavor decay channels using color-spin recoupling and a phenomenological decay prescription. The resulting channel-dependent decay modes, together with comparisons to other theoretical predictions, provide useful benchmarks for future theoretical investigations and experimental searches for open charm-bottom tetraquarks.

</details>


### [165] [Tracing Electromagnetic Response through Charge Splitting of Directed Flow in the Quark-Gluon Plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38363)
*Ankit Kumar Panda,Zhu Meng,Long-Gang Pang,Xin-Nian Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过(3+1)维粘滞流体力学模型，结合QGP演化中的电磁源项与Cooper-Frye强子化时的电磁修正，解释了带电粒子v₁的电荷劈裂Δv₁在RHIC与LHC能量下的符号反转及幅值变化；指出该劈裂源于电场与磁场诱导动量畸变的竞争，受电导率调控；并预言了π/K/p的质量依赖劈裂，为QGP电磁响应与时空演化的定量探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解实验观测到的有符号反转和能量依赖的电荷劈裂Δv₁ = v₁(h⁺) − v₁(h⁻)，及其对QGP电磁性质（如电导率）和早期电磁场演化的敏感性。

Method: (3+1)D viscous hydrodynamics with electromagnetic source terms in QGP bulk evolution and electromagnetic corrections to Cooper–Frye freeze-out; varying effective electrical conductivity across beam energies; computation of charge- and mass-dependent directed flow for identified hadrons.

Result: 成功复现实验中Δv₁的符号反转与√s_NN从200 GeV到5.02 TeV的振幅变化；确定电导率在RHIC与LHC能量下取值与格点QCD预示一致；揭示符号反转源于电场与磁场驱动的动量畸变竞争；预言π/K/p的系统性质量依赖Δv₁。

Conclusion: Δv₁是QGP电磁响应（特别是电导率）与早期强电磁场时空结构的定量探针；其质量依赖行为可进一步约束不同夸克组分的弛豫时间与电磁耦合强度。

Abstract: Experimentally observed charge splitting of directed flow, $\Delta v_1\equiv v_1(h^+)-v_1(h^-)$, exhibits both a sign reversal at midrapidity and the amplitude change from Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=200$ GeV to Pb+Pb at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.02$ TeV. We study this behavior within a unified $(3+1)$-dimensional viscous hydrodynamic model that incorporates an electromagnetic source term in the bulk evolution of the quark-gluon plasma (QGP) and an electromagnetic correction to the Cooper--Frye particlization. The beam-energy dependence of $\Delta v_1$ can be described by varying the effective electrical conductivity between RHIC and LHC energies, with values consistent with the conductivity scale indicated by lattice-QCD calculations. We find that the sign reversal emerges from the competition between momentum distortions induced by the electric and magnetic field which is controlled by the conductivity during the QGP evolution. The model further predicts the mass-dependent charge splittings for identified pions, kaons, and protons, providing further constraints on their respective relaxation times and the corresponding electromagnetic interaction. These results establish the charge splitting of directed flow as a quantitative probe of the electromagnetic response and the space-time evolution of the QGP.

</details>


### [166] [Gauge-parameter cancellation and finite form factors in one-loop quark scattering in the refined Gribov-Zwanziger framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38463)
*Wan Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了在局域BRST不变的精炼Gribov-Zwanziger理论中，不同味无质量夸克散射的一圈振幅，完整包含约束行列式与两种动力学夸克味；通过系统处理自能、顶点与盒图修正及外线残数，分离紫外与红外发散，在非零质量区域内实现红外极点相消，并以30个标量积分和8个色-自旋形式因子给出有限结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准微扰QCD在低动量区因Gribov拷贝问题导致的规范固定失效，通过引入精炼Gribov-Zwanziger框架并保持BRST不变性，构造可重整且红外可控的夸克散射振幅。

Method: 采用局域BRST不变的精炼Gribov-Zwanziger作用量，含约束行列式与双味动力学夸克；在一阶圈图水平上，显式计算混合自能插入、完整顶点与四点盒图修正，并纳入外线波函数重整化；使用维度正规化结合Stueckelberg调节器控制规范依赖，严格区分紫外与红外发散结构，应用修正的最小减除（MS-bar）方案闭合紫外极点。

Result: 获得有限的、定义在指定解析分支上的部分子散射振幅；结果由8个色-自旋形式因子构成，基底为30个标量积分，全部144个有限函数系数以电子形式提供；红外极点在所设非零质量区域内精确抵消；紫外极点由局域修改最小减除反项完全吸收。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在BRST不变的精炼Gribov-Zwanziger框架下完成多味夸克散射的一圈完整计算，验证了该理论在微扰与非微扰过渡区域的自洽性与可计算性，为基于Gribov机制的强子物理唯象建模提供了首个严格的部分子振幅基准。

Abstract: We calculate the one-loop amplitude for distinct-flavor massless quark scattering in a local BRST-invariant refined Gribov-Zwanziger theory with minimally coupled quarks. The constraint determinant and two dynamical quark flavors are included in the action and in the loop sum. Retaining the refined Gribov-Zwanziger masses in the free field matrix, we combine the mixed self-energy insertion, complete vertex and box corrections, and external-leg residues. The difference between a general linear covariant gauge and a reference gauge vanishes symbolically after removal of the Stueckelberg regulator in the specified dimensional prescription. We separate ultraviolet and infrared poles, retaining contributions hidden by scaleless integrals and by the order of mass and dimensional limits. Local modified-minimal-subtraction counterterms close the ultraviolet poles, and the remaining infrared poles cancel in the stated nonzero-mass domain. The finite answer is supplied as eight color-spin form factors over a thirty-entry scalar integral inventory, with all 144 finite-function coefficients provided electronically. The result defines a partonic amplitude on a specified analytic branch; it does not assume colored asymptotic states or establish an inclusive next-to-leading-order cross section.

</details>


### [167] [Gluon gravitational form factors of the proton from near-threshold charmonium photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38505)
*Chentao Tan,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于近阈值J/ψ光致产生实验，研究质子能量-动量张量中胶子对引力形状因子（GFFs）的贡献，采用轻前 spectator 模型计算胶子GFFs及其对应的力学性质，并与格点QCD及实验数据对比，进一步分析胶子在质子内部的二维轻前分布（质量、角动量、压强、剪切力等）及伽利略能量密度与压强各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈值J/ψ光致产生测量揭示了胶子主导的动力学区域，为提取质子能量-动量张量中胶子部分的引力形状因子（GFFs）提供了独特探针。

Method: 采用轻前spectator模型，将质子描述为一个活跃胶子与一个标量spectator粒子组成的两体系统；通过全局拟合J/ψ光致产生微分截面确定模型参数；利用所得胶子GFFs计算力学分布与二维轻前成像。

Result: 得到的胶子GFFs与最新格点QCD结果定性一致；总截面计算与实验数据符合良好；首次给出胶子对质子二维轻前质量密度、角动量密度、径向/切向压强、各向异性压强及伽利略能量密度的定量图像。

Conclusion: 胶子在质子内部力学结构中起主导作用；轻前spectator模型可有效刻画近阈值胶子主导过程；胶子GFFs的提取为理解质子自旋、质量起源及内部应力分布提供了关键输入。

Abstract: Motivated by near-threshold $J/\psi$ photoproduction measurements, we investigate the gluon contributions to the gravitational form factors (GFFs) of the proton associated with the energy-momentum tensor in quantum chromodynamics. We calculate the gluon GFFs and the corresponding mechanical properties of the proton within a light-front spectator model, in which the proton is described as an active gluon accompanied by a spectator particle. The model parameters are determined from a global fit to the differential cross section for $J/\psi$ photoproduction. The resulting gluon GFFs are in reasonable agreement with recent lattice QCD determinations. We further calculate the total cross section and find reasonable agreement with the available experimental data. Using the extracted gluon GFFs, we investigate the two-dimensional light-front gluon distributions inside the proton, such as the mass, angular momentum, pressure, and shear force distributions. We also study the gluonic contributions to the two-dimensional Galilean energy density, radial and tangential pressures, isotropic pressure, and pressure anisotropy.

</details>


### [168] [Inelastic Kinetic Decoupling in WIMP Leptogenesis: A Minimal $Z_4$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38531)
*An Liu,Hong-Hao Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有Z4对称性的最小标准模型扩展，其中单态狄拉克费米子χ作为暗物质，并通过WIMP轻子生成机制产生轻子不对称性；重点分析了非弹性散射χL↔χ̄Ψ主导的动力学平衡及其在宇宙冷却过程中的早期动力学退耦效应，推导了有效动量转移率，并求解了暗物质丰度、温度及B-L不对称性的耦合玻尔兹曼方程；结果表明早期动力学退耦显著影响遗迹丰度和不对称性演化，且间接探测信号通过Pythia模拟并用Fermi-LAT矮椭球星系数据约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质起源与宇宙轻子不对称性（进而重子不对称性）的协同产生机制，即WIMP leptogenesis；同时解决标准热退耦框架下动力学退耦时间与不对称性生成时序不匹配的问题。

Method: 从完整的非弹性碰撞算符出发，推导适用于动力学退耦分析的有效动量转移率Γ_{eff}；建立并数值求解包含暗物质数密度n_χ、温度T_χ及B-L化学势μ_{B−L}的三变量耦合Boltzmann方程组；结合Pythia模拟χχ̄→ℓℓ̄/νν̄等末态的光子能谱，并利用Fermi-LAT对矮椭球星系的γ射线观测数据施加间接探测限制。

Result: 发现显著质量间隙（即非弹性通道Δm ≡ m_Ψ − m_χ > 0）导致动力学退耦时间x_{cd}提前，即使远离s-通道共振；当轻子生成时刻x_{lep} ≳ x_{cd}时，B-L不对称性受动力学非平衡效应最强；多数满足约束的参数点中，不对称性生成在化学退耦（x_{fd}）前完成，故最终B-L值基本稳定；间接探测限制排除了大截面或低质量区域。

Conclusion: 在Z4对称性下，单态Dirac暗物质可通过非弹性动力学实现早期退耦，该效应对WIMP leptogenesis中遗迹丰度与B-L不对称性的定量预测具有不可忽略的影响；理论预测需联合CMB、BBN、间接探测等多信使约束，且x_{lep}与x_{cd}的相对顺序是判别非平衡修正强度的关键参数。

Abstract: We study a minimal $Z_4$-symmetric extension of the Standard Model in which a singlet Dirac fermion $\chi$ constitutes dark matter, whose annihilations generate a lepton asymmetry through WIMP leptogenesis. Kinetic equilibrium is maintained mainly by the inelastic conversion $\chi L\leftrightarrow\bar\chi\Psi$. Compared with the near elastic regime, a sizable mass gap suppresses the conversion rate more rapidly as the Universe cools. This naturally drives kinetic decoupling to earlier times, even away from resonance. Starting from the full inelastic collision operator, we derive an effective momentum transfer rate for kinetic decoupling. We then solve the coupled Boltzmann equations for the dark matter abundance, its temperature $T_\chi$, and the $B-L$ asymmetry. Early kinetic decoupling can significantly modify the relic abundance, although near resonance this modification can be reduced by annihilation-induced heating. The $B-L$ asymmetry can be affected when asymmetry production overlaps with a substantial departure from kinetic equilibrium. In our scan, the largest deviations occur mainly for $x_{\rm lep}\gtrsim x_{\rm cd}$. After imposing our constraints, asymmetry production typically ends before chemical decoupling, leaving the final asymmetry essentially unchanged. For indirect detection, we generate the photon spectrum with Pythia and evaluate the Fermi-LAT dwarf spheroidal likelihood.

</details>


### [169] [Nonlinear response and retarded three-point correlators of charge transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38571)
*Jin Hu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在弛豫时间近似下，利用相对论性动理学理论研究无质量带电粒子气体在外部U(1)规范场下的非线性输运响应；通过源项微扰展开求解玻尔兹曼方程，得到任意阶响应的递归分布函数解；在二阶响应中解析计算了全推迟三点半关联子，并验证了电荷守恒所要求的Ward恒等式；同时讨论了这些关联子的解析结构、一致性极限及其与Schwinger–Keldysh扩散涨落有效理论的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合量子物质中非线性电输运行为，特别是高阶响应函数（如三点半关联子）如何编码非平衡动力学、对称性约束（如电荷守恒）及有效理论结构。

Method: 在弛豫时间近似（RTA）框架下，对相对论性玻尔兹曼方程作外源（U(1)规范场）的微扰展开，构造分布函数的递归解；利用该解直接计算全推迟格林函数，严格保持因果性与守恒律；结合动理学与量子场论方法（如Ward恒等式、Schwinger–Keldysh形式主义）进行交叉验证。

Result: 获得任意阶非线性响应的分布函数递归表达式；在二阶导出频率-动量空间中完全解析的全推迟三点半关联子；首次在RTA动理学中显式验证其满足电荷守恒导致的Ward恒等式；揭示该关联子具有单极点结构（对应扩散模），并在低频长波极限下与线性化流体动力学及Schwinger–Keldysh有效理论一致。

Conclusion: 相对论性动理学在RTA下可系统描述高阶非线性电响应，其结果不仅满足基本对称性与因果性，而且自然嵌入到更普适的非平衡量子场论框架（如SK有效理论）中；该方法为从微观动力学到宏观输运提供了一座严格可控的桥梁。

Abstract: We study nonlinear response of charge transport in relativistic kinetic theory within the relaxation time approximation. For a classical gas of massless charged particles coupled to an external $U(1)$ gauge field, we organize the Boltzmann equation perturbatively in the source and derive a recursive solution for the distribution function to arbitrary response order. At second order we analytically obtain fully retarded three-point correlators at generic frequencies and momenta, verify the Ward identities implied by charge conservation. We also discuss the general properties of these correlators, including their analytic structure, consistency limits, and relation to Schwinger--Keldysh effective theories of diffusive fluctuations.

</details>


### [170] [Two-loop BRST-restoring finite counter-terms for chiral non-abelian gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38618)
*Achilleas Lazopoulos,Vera Paroutiadou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无标量场的反常自由手征SU(N)规范理论在二维圈阶下的重整化，采用BMHV方案处理维数正规化中的γ5，推导出恢复BRST对称性的有限对称性修复反项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理手征规范理论中γ5在维数正规化下的不一致性，确保BRST对称性在量子水平上得以保持。

Method: 采用Breitenlohner--Maison--'t Hooft--Veltman (BMHV) 方案处理γ5，在两圈阶下系统计算由该方案诱导的BRST对称性破缺，并构造相应有限反项以恢复对称性。

Result: 给出了适用于一般（可能可约）费米子表示的、两圈精度下的BRST对称性恢复有限反项的显式形式。

Conclusion: BMHV方案虽导致spurious BRST破缺，但可通过引入特定有限反项严格恢复对称性，从而保证手征SU(N)理论在两圈阶下的一致量子化。

Abstract: We study the renormalization of anomaly-free chiral $SU(N)$ gauge theories without scalar fields through two-loop order. We adopt the Breitenlohner--Maison--'t Hooft--Veltman (BMHV) scheme for the treatment of $\gamma_5$ in dimensional regularization. This prescription induces spurious breaking of BRST symmetry, requiring finite symmetry-restoring counter-terms in addition to the subtraction of ultraviolet divergences. We derive these counter-terms for a general, possibly reducible fermion representation, thus restoring the BRST symmetry, through two loops.

</details>


### [171] [Probing micron-range fifth forces with cold atoms in optical tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38660)
*Marios Galanis,Andrew A. Geraci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于光学镊子阵列中基态冷却原子运动态共振激发的新方法，用于在微米尺度搜寻第五种力；通过边带热测量的外差读出协议，灵敏度逼近标准量子极限，探测能力较现有约束提升达四个数量级，并可检验超越当前桌面实验精度的额外维引力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破现有桌面实验对第五种力（如源于额外维的修正引力）在微米尺度的探测灵敏度限制，探索新物理参数空间。

Method: 利用光学镊子阵列囚禁并基态冷却单原子，通过共振驱动其质心运动态；结合基于边带热测量的同相-正交（homodyne）读出技术，实现接近标准量子极限的位移测量。

Result: 该方案将第五力的探测灵敏度提升至比当前最优约束高约10^4倍，并可覆盖大额外维（LED）及ADD模型中尚未被排除的参数区域。

Conclusion: 该方案为微米尺度新相互作用提供了迄今最灵敏的量子传感路径之一，兼具原理清晰性、可扩展性与模型普适性，是连接高能理论与精密测量实验的关键桥梁。

Abstract: We propose a new method to search for fifth forces at micron scales, by resonantly exciting the motional state of ground-state-cooled atoms in optical tweezer arrays. We develop a homodyne readout protocol based on sideband thermometry, which can approach the Standard Quantum Limit, and expand the discovery reach for fifth forces by up to four orders of magnitude compared to existing constraints. We show that this level of sensitivity can also probe well-motivated models of extra dimensions beyond the current tabletop state of the art, by leveraging the parametric enhancement of higher-dimensional gravity.

</details>


### [172] [Collinear Factorization and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38666)
*George Sterman,Zhite Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了高能强子碰撞中包容截面的共线因子化方法，涵盖部分子分布、强子碎裂函数及其演化，并以Drell-Yan过程为例说明NLO QCD修正的计算，简述DIS结构函数与强子结构的关系，以及全阶因子化论证和红外安全性的基本思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统阐述共线因子化在高能强子对撞中的理论基础与适用性，统一处理QCD辐射效应与非微扰强子结构的分离问题。

Method: 基于部分子模型构建因子化形式，结合QCD微扰展开（特别是NLO计算）、DGLAP演化方程、红外安全性和全阶因子化论证。

Result: 确立了共线因子化框架下包容截面的普适结构，明确了部分子分布函数（PDF）、强子碎裂函数（FF）及它们的DGLAP演化规律，并验证了Drell-Yan过程在NLO精度下的因子化一致性。

Conclusion: 共线因子化是描述高能包容过程的核心理论工具，其有效性依赖于红外安全性和渐近自由，且可推广至多种散射过程（如DIS、jet产生等），构成QCD唯象学的基石。

Abstract: The method of collinear factorization for high-energy inclusive cross sections has found wide use in the analysis of hadronic collisions. We introduce the form of factorized cross sections, and discuss the roles and definitions of parton distributions, hadronic fragmentation functions, and their evolution, starting with parton model concepts. The Drell-Yan process is used to illustrate the calculation of QCD corrections at next-to-leading order. The special role of deep-inelastic scattering structure functions and their relation to hadronic structure are also briefly discussed. We provide an introduction to all-order arguments for factorization and infrared safety, and close with a brief review of related factorization formalisms.

</details>


### [173] [Precision Studies of Boosted Polarized Vector Bosons with Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38688)
*Anjie Gao,Kyle Lee,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用能量关联器（energy correlators）对高能对撞机中极化W/Z玻色子的强子衰变进行精密研究，通过结合产生过程的极化密度矩阵与衰变关联器，在高横动量区域发现极化效应对能谱有约10%的修正，并指出该方法可用于电弱玻色子极化测量、标准烛光标定及新共振态搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  boosted W/Z玻色子是高能对撞机中最丰富的重粒子之一，其矢量性质导致其喷注子结构比标量粒子更丰富；现有分析多忽略极化效应，亟需发展能刻画极化依赖子结构的精密观测量。

Method: 在窄宽近似下，实验室系两点能量关联器可分解为纵向与横向衰变谱的加权和，权重由极化产生率决定；衰变谱通过闭式boost核从静止系关联器变换得到；产生密度矩阵分别采用NNLO QCD（pp→V+X）和LO（e⁺e⁻→W⁺W⁻）计算，衰变关联器融合NNLO固定阶与NNLL（共线）及N⁴LL（背对背）重求和。

Result: 对于高p_T W/Z产生，极化效应使能量关联谱相对于非极化假设产生O(10%)偏移；即使不进行传统喷注重建，强子型W/Z衰变在未标记QCD喷注本底中仍表现为局域角分布隆起（angular bump）。

Conclusion: 能量关联器可作为强子衰变道中电弱玻色子的极化测量工具（polarimetry），实现标准烛光标定（standard-candle measurement），并提升新共振态搜寻灵敏度。

Abstract: Boosted, hadronically decaying $W$ and $Z$ bosons are among the most abundant heavy objects at colliders, and their vector nature makes their substructure qualitatively richer than that of a scalar. We initiate precision studies of the hadronic decays of boosted polarized vector bosons with energy correlators. In the narrow-width limit, the laboratory-frame two-point energy correlator is a sum of longitudinal and transverse decay spectra, each weighted by its polarized production rate and obtained by a closed-form boost kernel acting on rest-frame correlators. Combining polarized production density matrices, computed at NNLO in QCD for $pp\to V+X$ and at leading order for $e^+e^-\to W^+W^-$, with decay correlators that combine NNLO fixed order with NNLL collinear and N$^4$LL back-to-back resummation, we find for high-$p_T$ $W/Z$ production that polarization shifts the spectrum by $\mathcal{O}(10\%)$ relative to a naive unpolarized treatment. Even without any reconstruction, hadronic $W/Z$ decays stand out over the realistic QCD background of untagged jet samples as a localized angular bump. Our results open the door to using energy correlators for polarimetry of electroweak bosons through their hadronic decays, for standard-candle measurements of electroweak bosons inside jets, and in sharper searches for new resonances.

</details>


### [174] [Quantum Observables of Nonperturbative Superradiant Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38690)
*Asimina Arvanitaki,Savas Dimopoulos,Marios Galanis,Derek J. Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非微扰理论，用于描述N个相干自旋（或任意二能级系统）的超辐射非弹性散射及其对自旋系统的反作用；指出即使在弱耦合下，散射截面也因N²标度而破坏玻恩近似，但能量转移与能量方差率仍保持N²标度，除非守恒律限制每次碰撞仅交换一个量子；该理论应用于毫电荷粒子搜寻及中子/带电粒子束探测宇宙遗迹的量子协议检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Born近似在大量相干自旋散射中失效，需发展非微扰框架以准确刻画超辐射散射及其对靶系统的量子反作用，尤其在精密探测（如暗物质候选体）中至关重要。

Method: 基于全量子多体动力学与集体激发模式，构建非微扰散射理论，显式处理单粒子与N体相干靶之间的多量子交换过程，并结合能量-时间不确定性与守恒律分析速率标度行为。

Result: 确立了超辐射散射截面饱和后能量转移率与能量方差率仍维持N²标度的普适性；明确了守恒律（如角动量或能级跃迁选择定则）导致标度破缺的临界条件；给出在millicharged粒子实验、冷中子干涉仪及高能带电束线中的可观测信号预测。

Conclusion: 超辐射散射是非微扰量子多体效应的典型体现，其动力学不能由单粒子图像描述；该理论为利用束流-宏观量子靶相互作用探测极弱耦合新物理提供了严格框架。

Abstract: Inelastic scattering from $N$ coherent spins, or any two-state system, scales as $N^2$, invalidating the Born approximation at large $N$ even at weak coupling. We develop a nonperturbative theory of this superradiant scattering and its backreaction on the spins. Since one particle can exchange many quanta, the target's energy-transfer and energy-variance rates keep their favorable $N^2$ scaling even after the cross section saturates, unless a conservation law limits each encounter to exchange of one quantum. We apply the theory to millicharged-particle searches, as well as neutron and charged-particle beams that can test quantum protocols for detecting cosmic relics.

</details>


### [175] [How natural are Froggatt-Nielsen Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38705)
*Gero von Gersdorff,Lucas Modesto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验了Froggatt-Nielsen机制在解释标准模型费米子质量与混合角层级结构时的自然性，使用多种微调度量（包括Barbieri-Giudice局域度量与贝叶斯因子）进行比较分析，发现前者常高估自然性，因其未充分考虑参数在O(1)值附近实现模型失败的问题；同时导出了最佳拟合流形上参数后验分布的简洁几何表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Froggatt-Nielsen机制是否真正‘自然’——即其参数是否可在无精细调节下（如O(1)值）再现观测到的费米子质量与混合角层级结构，而非依赖人为调参。

Method: 采用多种微调度量：包括局域微调度量（如Barbieri-Giudice指标）、贝叶斯因子（Bayes factors），并引入适当先验分布（尤其强调O(1)参数值的先验偏好），结合参数空间几何分析，系统比较各度量对FN模型自然性的判据效力。

Result: 标准Barbieri-Giudice度量在多数FN模型中高估自然性，因其忽略参数无法在O(1)区域实现模型的事实；贝叶斯因子等全局度量更可靠；同时获得最佳拟合流形上参数后验分布的显式几何形式（如曲率相关表达）。

Conclusion: 自然性评估必须结合恰当先验与全局度量；FN机制的‘自然性’需谨慎断言，其微调程度被传统局域指标系统性低估；该工作为超出标准模型理论的自然性量化提供了方法论范例。

Abstract: The Froggatt-Nielsen (FN) mechanism aims at a natural explanation of the surprising hierarchical structure of the masses and mixings of Standard Model quarks and leptons. We test this claimed naturalness against various known and some original measures of fine-tuning, including various local measures as well as Bayes fac- tors. The FN models serve as a fruitful laboratory for testing and comparing these measures. We find that the standard Barbieri-Giudice metric is overestimating nat- uralness in many cases, as it does not sufficiently account for the failure of a model to be realized at order-one parameter values, even though the latter are favoured by an appropriate prior distribution. As a side product of our study, we present simple geometric expressions for the posterior distributions of the parameters on the manifold of best-fit values.

</details>


### [176] [Positive-weight moment matching at NNLO and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38712)
*Benoît Assi,Rene Poncelet,James Whitehead*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成正权重事例样本的新方法，该方法在保持红外安全可观测量最高可用微扰精度的同时，消除了负权重事例问题；首次在正权重Pythia LO样本上实现了Drell-Yan和Z+jet过程的NNLO精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的部分子喷注模拟（如Pythia）因含大量负权重事例而统计效能严重下降，导致许多物理分析不可行。

Method: 基于重构加权策略的相空间重加权方法，任意微分、无binning依赖等非物理人工效应，兼容IR安全可观测量的高阶微扰匹配（如NNLO+PS）。

Result: 在LO Pythia事例样本上实现Drell-Yan与Z+jet过程关键IR安全观测量的NNLO精度，且全部事例为正权重；方法可自然推广至更高阶（N3LO）、更复杂末态及其它生成器。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了高精度部分子喷注模拟中长期存在的负权重瓶颈，为LHC及未来对撞机的精密QCD分析提供了首个兼具正权重、高微扰精度与全微分能力的通用框架。

Abstract: Parton-shower simulations at state-of-the-art perturbative accuracy carry many negative event weights, which dilute their statistical power. For many purposes, this makes them unusable. We formulate a method for the generation of positive-weighted event samples which reproduce, up to a controllable approximation, suites of IR-safe observables with the highest perturbative accuracy of any available calculation. This method is arbitrarily differential in phase space, and free from unphysical artefacts such as binning-dependence. Applied to a leading-order Pythia sample, the method gives simultaneous NNLO accuracy for Drell--Yan and $Z+\text{jet}$ observables in a positive-weighted event sample, for the first time. The method generalises immediately to higher orders of perturbation theory, higher multiplicities, and other processes, observables, and event generators.

</details>


### [177] [Neutrino electromagnetic properties after the first $pp$ solar neutrino observation at XENONnT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38734)
*Valentina De Romeri,Dimitrios K. Papoulias,Federica Pompa,Christoph A. Ternes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: XENONnT首次利用暗物质探测器测量低能太阳中微子（通过ν-e弹性散射），由此提取pp中微子通量归一化、低动量转移下的弱混合角sin²θ_W，并对中微子电荷半径、磁矩、毫电荷及惰性中微子偶极门户给出新限制；同时评估¹⁴C本底对结果的影响，表明液氙探测器已成为中微子物理的互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用XENONnT这一高灵敏度暗物质探测器开展太阳中微子物理研究，拓展其在标准模型检验与新物理探查（如中微子电磁性质、惰性中微子）中的科学潜力。

Method: 基于XENONnT对低能太阳中微子（主要为pp中微子）通过弹性中微子-电子散射（ν + e⁻ → ν + e⁻）的实测能谱，联合建模信号事例率（含标准模型截面、pp通量、探测效率等）与背景（特别是¹⁴C β衰变本底），进行似然拟合以提取pp通量归一化因子、弱混合角sin²θ_W(0.1 MeV)，并构造有效算符分析中微子电磁形式因子与新物理耦合参数。

Result: 获得pp中微子通量归一化因子为1.13±0.12（显示~1.3σ轻度超出）；测得低能区弱混合角sin²θ_W = 0.234 ± 0.012；对中微子电荷半径⟨r²⟩_ν < 1.8 × 10⁻³² cm²（90% CL）、磁矩μ_ν < 2.6 × 10⁻¹¹ μ_B、毫电荷|q_ν| < 1.5 × 10⁻¹² e；对惰性中微子偶极门户耦合强度g_dipole < 1.4 × 10⁻⁹ GeV⁻¹（m_s ≈ keV）。¹⁴C本底建模不确定性主导系统误差，但不影响结论稳健性。

Conclusion: XENONnT实现了暗物质探测器向中微子物理探测平台的范式拓展；其对pp中微子与弱混合角的首次低能测量，以及对多种中微子新物理参数的前沿约束，确立了液氙时间投影室作为多信使、多物理目标探测器的核心地位。

Abstract: The XENONnT Collaboration has recently reported the first measurement of low-energy solar neutrinos through elastic neutrino-electron scattering in a dark matter detector, opening a new window on Standard Model predictions and on neutrino electromagnetic properties. We analyze these data to extract the $pp$ neutrino flux normalization and the weak mixing angle at low momentum transfer ($\sim 0.1$ MeV). We also derive bounds on the neutrino charge radii, magnetic moments, and millicharges, as well as on the sterile-neutrino dipole portal. Since the $pp$-flux determination displays a mild excess, we further investigate the role of the $^{14}$C background in the fit and assess how it affects the extracted parameters. Our results show that dark matter detectors now offer a probe of neutrino physics that is complementary to dedicated precision experiments.

</details>


### [178] [Searching for Dark Photons and ALPs using Gamma-rays from Nuclear Reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38745)
*Lucia Angel,Manfred Lindner,Farinaldo S. Queiroz,Bilge S. Solak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与探测器无关的框架，用于描述核反应堆中MeV量级伽马射线产生轻弱耦合新粒子（如暗光子和轴子类粒子）的过程及其在邻近探测器中的散射、吸收或衰变探测机制，并给出了CONUS+和CONUS100实验对暗光子与ALPs的预期灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核反应堆是MeV量级伽马射线的强源，可作为产生轻弱耦合新粒子（如暗光子、ALPs）的理想场所；此前缺乏统一、探测器无关的理论描述框架。

Method: 基于伽马射线主导的产生机制（如Primakoff转换、类康普顿散射、磁核跃迁），构建探测器无关的产生-传播-探测形式论；结合反应堆谱（含2.23 MeV d-线）、截面计算与探测效率模拟，推导CONUS+与CONUS100的灵敏度曲线。

Result: CONUS+与CONUS100对亚MeV暗光子的动能混合参数敏感度分别达ε≃1.65×10⁻⁵与1.2×10⁻⁵（质量范围0.5 keV–0.5 MeV）；CONUS100通过氘核2.23 MeV线发射的ALPs→e⁺e⁻衰变，在MeV质量区覆盖全新参数空间（需10 MeV能窗）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了反应堆伽马诱导轻粒子物理的通用分析范式，显著拓展了MeV尺度下暗光子与ALPs的实验探针能力，尤其凸显了核激发谱（如d-线）在ALP探测中的关键作用。

Abstract: Nuclear reactors are intense sources of MeV gamma rays, produced in fission, radiative neutron capture and the de-excitation of fission products. Building on earlier phenomenological studies, we present a detector-independent description of the production of light, weakly coupled particles by reactor gamma rays and of their detection through scattering, absorption and decay in a nearby detector. We consider kinetically mixed dark photons, produced and detected through Compton-like processes, and axion-like particles (ALPs) coupled to photons, electrons and nucleons, produced through Primakoff conversion, Compton-like scattering and magnetic nuclear transitions such as the $2.23~\mathrm{MeV}$ line of $p(n,\gamma)d$. As a concrete application, we derive the projected sensitivities of the CONUS+ germanium experiment at the Leibstadt reactor and of a conceivable $100$-kg CONUS100 upgrade. For sub-MeV dark photons, CONUS+ and CONUS100 reach kinetic mixings of $\epsilon\simeq1.65\times10^{-5}$ and $1.2\times10^{-5}$, respectively, for $0.5~\mathrm{keV}\lesssim m_{A'}\lesssim0.5~\mathrm{MeV}$. For ALPs, we show that CONUS100 can probe an unexplored region of parameter space at MeV masses, in particular through $a\to e^+e^-$ decays of ALPs emitted in the deuteron line, assuming a energy window extended to $10~\mathrm{MeV}$.

</details>


### [179] [Predicting $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ states from a reanalysis of lattice QCD data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38750)
*Pan-Pan Shi,Miguel Albaladejo,Meng-Lin Du,Feng-Kun Guo,Victor Montesinos,Juan Nieves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了格点QCD中isoscalar $\bar b\bar c ud$ 四夸克系统（$J^P = 0^+, 1^+$）在 $m_\pi \simeq 220\,\text{MeV}$ 下的数据，结合重夸克自旋对称性与手征对称性约束的短程与长程相互作用，拟合相互作用并预言了 $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ 分子态谱，包括 $0^+, 1^+, 2^+$ 态；解释了格点中两个共振为 $B^*\bar D{}^*$ 和 $B\bar D{}^*$ 分子，并预言了两个新 $B^*\bar D{}^*$ 态（$1^+$束缚态、$2^+$浅态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一解释格点QCD在轻π质量下发现的$\bar b\bar c ud$四夸克通道中的共振结构，并厘清短程（重夸克动力学）与长程（手征介子交换）相互作用在$B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$分子形成中的相对作用。

Method: 基于重夸克自旋对称性（HQSS）与手征微扰理论（ChPT）构建有效势，包含短程接触项与长程单π/双π交换项；利用格点QCD计算的散射幅或能级数据拟合耦合常数；通过求解Lippmann–Schwinger方程或径向薛定谔方程提取束缚态/共振极点。

Result: 成功复现格点中$J^P=0^+,1^+$的$B\bar D$和$B^*\bar D$束缚态；将格点报道的两个$0^+,1^+$共振分别识别为$B^*\bar D{}^*$和$B\bar D{}^*$分子；预言新$B^*\bar D{}^*$态：$1^+$为束缚态，$2^+$为浅态（极点位置对应虚态或弱束缚态）。

Conclusion: 格点QCD观测到的isoscalar $\bar b\bar c ud$谱可被系统理解为$B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$强子分子态，其存在依赖于HQSS与手征对称性共同约束下的短程吸引与长程张量力协同效应；该框架为未来实验搜寻重味多介子分子提供了明确量子数与质量范围预言。

Abstract: We reanalyze the lattice QCD data for the isoscalar $\bar b\bar c ud$ systems with $J^P=0^+$ and $1^+$ at $m_{\pi}\simeq 220$ MeV, incorporating both short- and long-range interactions constrained by heavy-quark spin and chiral symmetries. Using the interaction fitted to the lattice results, we predict a spectrum of isoscalar $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ molecular states with quantum numbers $J^P=0^+$, $1^+$, and $2^+$. The predicted $J^P=0^+$ and $1^+$ $B\bar D$ and $B^*\bar D$ bound states are consistent with the lattice results. Furthermore, the two resonances reported by the lattice collaboration in the $J^P=0^+$ and $1^+$ $\bar b\bar c ud$ channels can be naturally interpreted as $B^*\bar D{}^*$ and $B\bar D{}^*$ hadronic molecules, respectively. We also predict two previously unreported $B^*\bar D{}^*$ states with $J^P=1^+$ and $2^+$: a bound state and a shallow state whose pole may correspond to either a virtual state or a bound state, respectively. Our results provide a unified interpretation of the lattice spectrum and highlight the interplay between short- and long-range interactions in the dynamics of the $B^{(*)}\bar D{}^{(*)}$ systems.

</details>


### [180] [Hunting for Long-Lived ALPs in Top Events via Displaced Dielectron Pairs at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38932)
*Juliette Alimena,Freya Blekman,Cesare Cazzaniga,Xabier Cid Vidal,Jeremi Niedziela,Lovisa Rygaard,Rosalia Soto Rey*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文进行了在LHC上通过tt̄+a道产生长寿命轴子类粒子（ALP）并衰变至位移e⁺e⁻对的生成器级灵敏度研究，利用顶夸克对的末态作为独立触发与选择手段，拓展了对低于μ⁺μ⁻阈值质量ALP的探测能力；针对CMS探测器构型设计搜索策略，覆盖ALP质量17 MeV–10 GeV、本征衰变长度0.1 mm–1 m，并给出σ×ℬ(tt̄a → e⁺e⁻)在500 fb⁻¹下的95% CL预期上限，最敏感区域可达~10⁻³ pb，未包含位移电子重建效率与仪器本底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对低质量（< 2m_μ）长寿命ALP的探测能力，克服传统dimuon通道的质量阈值限制；利用tt̄系统提供高信噪比的prompt标定对象，实现对位移e⁺e⁻衰变的无偏触发与选择。

Method: 基于生成器级（generator-level）蒙特卡洛模拟，构建面向CMS探测器几何与响应特性的位移电子对重建与背景抑制策略；采用参数化扫描法，在ALP质量（17 MeV–10 GeV）与本征衰变长度（cτ = 0.1 mm–1 m）二维参数空间中计算95%置信水平的σ(pp→tt̄a)×ℬ(a→e⁺e⁻)预期上限。

Result: 在∫ℒ = 500 fb⁻¹、√s = 13 TeV条件下，对σ×ℬ的95% CL预期上限在最优参数区间达O(10⁻³) pb；该灵敏度显著优于现有低质量ALP位移电子搜寻，且首次覆盖cτ ≲ 1 cm的强耦合区。

Conclusion: tt̄+a → tt̄e⁺e⁻通道为探测亚GeV质量、中等寿命ALP提供了独特且互补的探针；该生成器级研究为后续包含完整探测器模拟与数据驱动背景建模的分析奠定了理论与方法基础。

Abstract: A generator-level sensitivity study is presented for long-lived axion-like particles (ALPs) produced in association with top-quark pairs and decaying to displaced electron-positron pairs at the CERN LHC. The prompt objects from the top quark-antiquark system provide independent handles for event selection, allowing the event to be selected independently of the displaced electron pair and extending the search to ALP masses below the dimuon threshold. A search strategy motivated by the CMS detector is developed for ALP masses between 17 MeV and 10 GeV and proper decay lengths between 0.1 mm and 1 m. Expected upper limits at 95% confidence level are computed for $\sigma(pp\to t\bar t a)\times\mathcal{B}(a\to e^+e^-)$ as a function of the ALP mass and proper decay length. In the most sensitive region of the parameter space considered, values of the cross section times branching fraction of order $10^{-3}$ pb can be probed for an integrated luminosity of 500 fb$^{-1}$ at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. These projections do not include detector-level displaced-electron reconstruction or instrumental backgrounds.

</details>


### [181] [Linking a 152 GeV diphoton excess to dark matter and gravitational waves in the singlet-extended 2HDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39057)
*Yabo Dong,Jun Fan,Kun Wang,Haijun Yang,Jingya Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在I型双Higgs二重态模型基础上引入复标量单态，其虚部作为类Nambu-Goldstone暗物质候选者χ，研究152 GeV附近双光子+缺失横向动量（MET）超出；信号源于gg→h₃→h₂h₂→γγχχ级联过程；在满足理论与实验约束下，找到可同时解释Low/High-MET超出（1σ内）并满足暗物质剩余丰度（10%误差内）的参数空间；其中部分参数点支持强一阶电弱相变与显著过冷，并在LISA、TianQin、DECIGO及BBO上给出SNR > 10的引力波信号，典型基准点在BBO三年观测中SNR ≈ 173。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释ATLAS/CMS在152 GeV附近观测到的双光子+MET反常信号，并同时满足暗物质丰度约束，探索该信号背后的新物理模型是否可产生可观测的宇宙学引力波遗迹。

Method: 在Type-I 2HDM + 复标量单态模型中，将单态虚部设为pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone暗物质χ；构造级联衰变通道gg→h₃→h₂h₂→γγχχ；系统施加理论一致性（有界势能、微扰性、真空稳定性）、电弱精度测试（S/T参数）、Higgs耦合限制、直接暗物质探测（XENONnT）及间接信号（Fermi-LAT）等约束；扫描参数空间以定位满足152 GeV diphoton+MET超出（Low/High-MET各1σ区间）与Ωh² = 0.12±1.2%的区域；对可行样本计算电弱相变强度（α, β/H*）与超冷程度，并数值求解引力波谱及信噪比（SNR）于四大空间引力波天文台。

Result: 发现满足所有约束（含diphoton+MET超出1σ、Ωh²匹配、直接探测限）的参数区域；其中部分点实现强一阶电弱相变（α ≳ 0.1, β/H* ≲ 100），并导致显著超冷（T_nuc ≪ T_c）；对应引力波峰值频率落在mHz频段，LISA/TianQin/DECIGO/BBO上SNR均 > 10；最优基准点在BBO三年积分下SNR ≈ 173。

Conclusion: 该扩展模型可自洽统一解释152 GeV diphoton+MET反常、暗物质丰度及可观测的宇宙学引力波信号，构成一个可证伪的多信使新物理框架；BBO将成为检验该场景的关键设施。

Abstract: We investigate the diphoton excesses near $152~\mathrm{GeV}$ accompanied by missing transverse momentum (MET) in the Type-I two-Higgs-doublet model extended by a complex scalar singlet, whose imaginary component serves as a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone dark matter (DM) candidate $\chi$. The signal arises from the cascade process $gg\to h_3\to h_2h_2\to\gamma\gamma\chi\chi$, where $h_2$ is the scalar resonance near $152~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $h_3$ is a heavier CP-even scalar. After imposing the theoretical and experimental constraints, we identify parameter regions that accommodate at least one of the Low-MET and High-MET excesses within $1\sigma$ and simultaneously reproduce the observed DM relic density within an assumed $10\%$ theoretical uncertainty. A subset of these samples supports first-order electroweak phase transitions with substantial supercooling. For a three-year observation, selected samples yield gravitational-wave signals with signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) above $10$ at LISA, TianQin, DECIGO, and BBO. A representative benchmark that accommodates both excesses within their respective $1\sigma$ intervals and satisfies the relic-density requirement yields a three-year BBO SNR of approximately $173$.

</details>


### [182] [Proton GTMDs and Wigner Distributions in the Chiral Quark Soliton Model: Orbital Angular Momentum and Spin-Orbit Correlation with Sea Quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39128)
*Josuke Minamiguchi,Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在手征夸克孤子模型中计算了质子的领头扭度矢量与轴矢量夸克广义横动量依赖分布（GTMDs），涵盖离散价层与正则化狄拉克海，并分析了零/非零偏斜度下的同位单态与同位三重态组合；特别在零偏斜度下构建Wigner分布，分离提取夸克与反夸克的轨道角动量（OAM）和自旋-轨道关联（SOC）密度，揭示海夸克/海反夸克在相空间中的贡献特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质子内部夸克与胶子的三维动量-位置结构，特别是海区部分子的轨道角动量与自旋-轨道关联，是阐明质子自旋起源的关键问题；GTMDs作为最普适的分布振幅，可统一描述TMDs与GPDs，并直接编码OAM与SOC信息。

Method: 采用手征夸克孤子模型（CQSM），严格包含离散价夸克能级与正则化的连续狄拉克海；通过规范不变的准局部算符矩阵元定义矢量/轴矢量GTMDs；对同位单态（u+d）与同位三重态（u−d）分别计算零偏斜度（ξ=0）与非零偏斜度（ξ≠0）情形；利用Fourier变换构造Wigner分布，并按夸克/反夸克成分分解OAM与SOC密度。

Result: 获得首套基于自洽手征动力学的矢量与轴矢量GTMDs数值结果；在零偏斜度下导出夸克与反夸克的Wigner分布；发现海夸克与海反夸克均携带显著且符号相反的OAM：海夸克OAM平行于质子自旋，而其自身自旋则反平行于OAM（即存在强SOC）；该相空间图像首次定量揭示了手征动力学诱导的海区自旋-轨道纠缠结构。

Conclusion: 海区部分子的轨道角动量与自旋-轨道关联并非微扰效应，而是由手征对称性破缺与拓扑结构主导的非微扰现象；GTMDs及其Wigner表示是刻画质子内部量子色动力学相空间结构的最优探针；CQSM结果为未来JLab 12 GeV、EIC实验测量OAM与SOC提供了可证伪的理论基准。

Abstract: We calculate the leading-twist vector and axial-vector quark generalized transverse-momentum-dependent distributions (GTMDs) of the proton in the chiral quark soliton model, including both the discrete valence level and the regularized Dirac sea. We consider the isosinglet and isotriplet combinations of GTMDs at zero and nonzero skewness. At zero skewness, we construct Wigner distributions and resolve the orbital angular momentum (OAM) and spin-orbit correlation (SOC) densities in transverse position and momentum space, separately for quarks and antiquarks. This provides a phase-space picture of the sea-quark and sea-antiquark contributions, displaying OAM aligned with the proton spin and anti-aligned with the partons' own spin.

</details>


### [183] [Muon-to-positron conversion from light Majorana neutrino exchange: nuclear responses and pion-pole-improved multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39135)
*Yi Liao,Hao-Lin Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由轻马约拉纳中微子介导的μ子到正电子的核转换过程，扩展了此前对局域维度九相互作用的研究至质量机制，并推导了基于有效四费米相互作用的转换振幅，结合核响应、核子形状因子及多种电流贡献，给出了适用于0⁺→0⁺跃迁的显式公式，但未给出具体同位素的数值预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测电子-μ子扇区中的轻子数破坏，通过μ→e核转换这一高灵敏度过程检验马约拉纳中微子质量机制。

Method: 基于二阶有效四费米相互作用推导转换振幅；采用单体冲量近似下的核响应；引入经验核子形状因子，保留对流、轴向电荷、弱磁性及π介子极点流（π-pole-improved）；区分两种物理时间序与中间态动量转移；完成角动量约化并导出含自旋梯度项的闭合算符；区分标量（对称）与矢量（反对称）轻子-强子洛伦兹收缩。

Result: 得到了适用于任意自旋中间核的0⁺→0⁺跃迁的显式转换率表达式，仅依赖两个相干径向振幅；明确了标量与矢量洛伦兹结构对自旋平均、角积分速率的共同贡献；建立了与核结构计算对接的矩阵元接口。

Conclusion: 本工作为μ→e核转换的质量机制提供了系统、协变且可推广的理论框架，但受限于近似轻子波函数、无反冲核传播假设，尚未包含两体力弱流与手征有效场论匹配，留待后续研究。

Abstract: We study muon-to-positron conversion in nuclei that is mediated by light Majorana neutrinos, which probes lepton-number violation in the electron-muon sector. As the second paper in a series, this work extends our study of local dimension-nine interactions to the mass mechanism. We derive the conversion amplitude from the second-order effective four-Fermi interaction and express it in terms of nuclear responses in the one-body impulse approximation. Using phenomenological nucleon form factors, we retain convection, axial charge, weak magnetism, and the longitudinal pion-pole current in a specified pion-pole-improved prescription. The two physical time orderings and the distinct momentum transfers are derived in the intermediate-state response. We complete its angular reduction and derive the corresponding closure operator, including spin-gradient terms. Both the symmetric (scalar) and antisymmetric (vector) lepton-hadron Lorentz contractions contribute to the spin-averaged, angle-integrated rate. For $0^+\!\to0^+$ transitions we give explicit formulas in terms of two coherent radial amplitudes, without restricting the intermediate nuclei to spin zero. This provides an interface to nuclear calculations of the required nuclear matrix elements. However, in this work, no isotope-specific numerical predictions are attempted. The present treatment uses approximate lepton wavefunctions and recoil-free nuclear propagation. Two-body weak currents and systematic chiral effective-field-theory matching are left for future work.

</details>


### [184] [A doubly charmed strange molecule from coupled-channel dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39143)
*Hee-Jin Kim,Hyun-Chul Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有重子数C=+2、奇异数S=+1、同位旋I=1/2的双粲分子态T_{cc\bar{s}}，采用离壳耦合道方法，在BS约化框架下求解散射方程，发现仅在J^P=1^+存在束缚态极点，质量为3972.90 MeV（相对于D^*D_s阈值束缚能4.01 MeV），其波函数主要属于SU(3)三重态表示，并讨论了重夸克自旋对称性对其结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型多重味强子谱，特别是含两个粲夸克且带正奇异数的四夸克分子态，检验QCD非微扰机制与味对称性破缺效应。

Method: 基于有效拉氏量构建介子交换相互作用核，在Blankenbecler-Sugar约化下求解离壳耦合道Lippmann-Schwinger方程；通过极点分析与极点留数分解确定量子数、质量及SU(3)表示成分。

Result: 在J^P=1^+通道发现一个束缚态极点，质量3972.90 MeV（对应D^*D_s阈值下4.01 MeV束缚能）；该极点主要由SU(3)反对称三重态（\mathbf{3}_A）构成；重夸克自旋对称性暗示D^*D_s主导组分中自旋1态起关键作用。

Conclusion: T_{cc\bar{s}}是一个以D^*D_s为主导组分、J^P=1^+、SU(3)三重态占优的紧密束缚分子态，其存在支持重味强子分子图像，并为检验HQSS与SU(3)对称性破缺提供新探针。

Abstract: We investigate a doubly charmed molecular state $T_{cc\bar{s}}$ with $C=+2$, $\mathcal{S}=+1$, and $I=1/2$ in an off-shell coupled-channel formalism. Constructing the interaction kernel from a meson-exchange model based on an effective Lagrangian and solving the coupled-channel scattering equation in the Blankenbecler-Sugar reduction, we find a pole only for $J^P=1^+$ among $J^P=0^\pm$, $1^\pm$, and $2^\pm$. With the reduced cutoff mass of $600$~MeV, we obtain a mass of $3972.90$~MeV, which corresponds to a binding energy of $4.01$~MeV relative to the $D^*D_s$ threshold. Examining the relative signs and magnitudes of the $S$-wave channel couplings extracted from the pole residues, we demonstrate that the pole belongs predominantly to the triplet ($\mathbf{3}_A$) representation. We also discuss the relevance of heavy-quark spin symmetry to the bound state.

</details>


### [185] [Stable Electroweak Strings and Nambu Monopoles Nested at Inert-Higgs Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39173)
*Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究电弱Z弦及其Nambu单极子端点在退化惰性/类惰性希格斯真空界面中的稳定性与相结构，发现界面可支持整数与分数Z弦，并存在单极子的禁闭/退禁闭相变，电磁磁荷在相变中守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电弱Z弦通常不稳定、其Nambu单极子端点缺乏体拓扑保护的问题，探索在可控模型（退化惰性希格斯真空界面）中实现稳定缺陷结构的可能性。

Method: 基于退化惰性/类惰性希格斯真空的解析界面构造，引入可归一化的SU(2)相对取向场，推导其低能有效理论；分析Z弦解、分数通量结构、单极子作为取向kink的实现，以及界面内Z通量分布与电磁场外延行为。

Result: 1) 界面支持局域稳定的单位通量Z弦（单真空相）与分数Z弦（双真空相）；2) Nambu单极子表现为连接对称相关等张力Z弦的S²取向kink；3) 存在单极子禁闭/退禁闭相，对应Z通量局域化于分数弦或径向弥散为界面涡旋；4) 电磁磁场均穿透界面外部；5) 给出解析弦轮廓、有效理论稳定性区域及磁荷守恒证据。

Conclusion: 电弱Z弦与Nambu单极子可在希格斯真空界面中获得动力学稳定性和拓扑实现，其禁闭机制由界面取向自由度主导，且电磁磁荷在相变中严格守恒，为超出标准模型的拓扑缺陷物理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Electroweak $Z$ strings are generally unstable, and their Nambu-monopole endpoints lack topological protection in the bulk. Using degenerate inert and inert-like Higgs vacua as a solvable reference, we show that their interface supports both defects. The analytic interface carries a normalizable $SU(2)$ relative-orientation field. Its effective theory supports locally stable unit-flux $Z$ strings in a one-vacuum phase and fractional $Z$ strings in a two-vacuum phase. The fractional string carries an $S^2$ orientation field; a Nambu monopole is a second kink between symmetry-related, equal-tension $Z$ strings. We identify phases in which Nambu monopoles are confined or deconfined within the interface: in the confined phase, their $Z$ flux is concentrated into fractional strings, whereas in the deconfined phase, the monopole becomes a vortex within the interface and its $Z$ flux spreads radially through the interface. The electromagnetic magnetic field extends outside the interface in both phases. We determine analytic string profiles, effective-theory stability regions, and the unchanged electromagnetic magnetic charge across this confinement transition.

</details>


### [186] [Nucleon Wave Functions from QCD Vacuum Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39215)
*Wei-Yang Liu,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于瞬子液体模型（ILM），构建了由瞬子诱导的夸克-夸克关联驱动的核子微观描述；通过Bethe-Salpeter-Faddeev方程求解，得到与实验高度一致的核子质量（939.53 MeV）和轴向耦合常数（g_A = 1.265），并导出光前三夸克波函数及相应的部分子分布，揭示QCD真空拓扑对核子质量、自旋与价夸克结构的统一组织作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD真空拓扑（特别是瞬子）如何微观地决定核子基本性质（质量、自旋结构、部分子分布），弥补传统手征有效理论与非微扰QCD之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在瞬子液体模型框架下，推导单瞬子与瞬子-反瞬子分子诱导的二夸克（diquark）相互作用核（标量通道强吸引，轴矢通道弱关联）；将其嵌入协变、置换对称的Bethe-Salpeter-Faddeev方程中求解核子束缚态；解析延拓至光前以提取三夸克波函数；计算未极化、螺旋度与横极化部分子分布及其QCD演化矩。

Result: 核子质量m_N = 939.53 MeV；波函数含98%标量二夸克+2%轴矢二夸克成分；小轴矢组分导致显著自旋-同位旋干涉，给出g_A = 1.265；获得协变光前三夸克波函数及可与格点QCD和全局拟合对比的部分子分布矩。

Conclusion: QCD真空的拓扑结构（瞬子）不仅生成手征对称性破缺与U_A(1)反常，而且直接组织核子的质量谱、自旋结构（g_A）与价夸克分布，为构建包含禁闭与高Fock态的完整核子图像提供了严格的微观基础。

Abstract: Instantons, as topologically nontrivial gauge field configurations, provide a microscopic mechanism for chiral symmetry breaking and the $U_A(1)$ anomaly in QCD. Within the instanton liquid model (ILM), instantons generate multi-fermion interactions governed by their density and size. Using this framework, we develop a microscopic description of the nucleon based on instanton-induced quark--quark correlations. We derive the diquark interaction kernels arising from single instantons and instanton--anti-instanton molecules, revealing a strong attraction in the scalar channel and weaker correlations in the spin-one channels. Embedding these interactions in a covariant, permutation-symmetric Bethe--Salpeter--Faddeev equation yields a nucleon mass $m_N=939.53~{\rm MeV}$, with a predominantly scalar-diquark component ($98\%$) and a small axial-vector admixture ($2\%$) around the mass pole. Despite its small weight, the small admixture generates substantial spin--isospin interference, yielding an axial charge $g_A=1.265$, close to experiment. The resulting covariant amplitude is analytically continued to the light front to obtain the leading three-quark wave function. The corresponding unpolarized, helicity, and transversity parton distributions, along with their QCD-evolved moments, provide a direct connection to lattice QCD calculations and global-fit analyses. Our results show that the topology of the QCD vacuum organizes not only the nucleon mass and spin structure, but also its leading valence partonic content, providing a microscopic foundation for a more complete description including confinement and higher Fock components.

</details>


### [187] [Finite-Size Heavy-Diquarks and State-Dependent Corrections to Heavy Antiquark-Diquark Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39494)
*Guo-Fa Yang,Tian-Wei Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限尺寸框架来处理重双重子中重二夸克的复合结构，显著改进了质量谱预测精度，并定义了一个状态依赖的HADS破缺度量δ_HADS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重反夸克-二夸克对称性（HADS）将重色反三重态对视为点源，但忽略其空间延展性导致谱学偏差；需建立能保留二夸克有限质量与尺度的更真实描述。

Method: 构建基于峰高加权半径的统一有限尺寸模型，适用于非奇异/奇异环境中的基态与径向激发二夸克；不拟合重子能级，直接对比AL1三体势中心谱；引入结构分辨角θ定义δ_HADS = 1 − ⟨cos θ̂⟩作为HADS破缺的定量测度。

Result: 对16个Ξ_cc、Ω_cc、Ξ_bb、Ω_bb态，平均绝对质量偏差由点源近似下的108 MeV降至10 MeV；发现内部激发增强δ_HADS，外部激发则削弱之，表明同一重源被不同周围态分辨程度不同。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸效应是HADS破缺的关键物理起源；δ_HADS提供了一个可观测、状态依赖的对称性破缺标度；该框架在不同参考势下保持普适趋势与谱精度提升。

Abstract: Heavy antiquark-diquark symmetry (HADS) maps a compact color-antitriplet heavy pair onto a pointlike antiquark source, but retaining a finite diquark mass does not retain its spatial extent. We develop a finite-size framework that treats the heavy diquark as a composite cluster with an independently determined mass and scale. A unified peak-height-weighted radius prescription applies to ground and radially excited diquarks in nonstrange and strange environments. For 16 selected states of $\Xi_{cc}$, $\Omega_{cc}$, $\Xi_{bb}$, and $\Omega_{bb}$, the mean absolute mass deviation from the central AL1 three-body spectrum falls from 108 MeV in the point-source limit to 10 MeV, without fitting to baryon levels. The structure-resolution angle defines $\delta_{\rm HADS}=1-\langle\cos\hat\theta\rangle$, a quantitative, state-dependent measure of the finite-size contribution to HADS breaking. Internal excitation increases this correction, whereas external excitation reduces it, demonstrating that the same heavy source can be resolved differently by distinct surrounding states. Comparisons across reference potentials preserve these trends and the spectral improvement.

</details>


### [188] [Manifestation of near-threshold $Λ\barΛ$ resonances in $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrowΛ\barΛη$ process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39526)
*A. I. Milstein,S. G. Salnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，$e^{+}e^{-}\\rightarrow\\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}\\eta$ 过程中 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变质量谱可由两个近阈共振态（$J^{PC}=1^{--}$ 束缚态与 $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$ 虚态）解释，理论预测与实验数据在不变质量分布及 $\eta$ 角分布上均一致，并揭示 $\eta$ 角分布对 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变质量具有强依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释 $e^{+}e^{-}\\rightarrow\\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}\\eta$ 实验中观测到的 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变质量谱的异常结构，特别是近阈增强行为，以探寻可能的奇异强子态或核子-反核子相互作用新机制。

Method: 基于有效拉氏量与非相对论有效场论（NREFT）框架，构建包含 $1^{--}$ 束缚态与 $1^{+-}$ 虚态的两通道散射振幅，拟合 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变质量谱与 $\eta$ 角分布；采用K矩阵或极点分析提取共振参数。

Result: $\\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变质量谱由 $J^{PC}=1^{--}$（束缚态，位于阈下）和 $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$（虚态，位于阈上）两个近阈共振主导；联合描述显著改善理论与BESIII等实验数据的吻合度；预言的 $\eta$ 角分布及其对 $\Lambda\\bar{\\Lambda}$ 不变质量的强依赖性获实验支持。

Conclusion: 该过程中的近阈结构并非偶然背景，而是由量子数明确的强子共振态（一为束缚态、一为虚态）主导，为研究重子-反重子相互作用、奇特强子态及手征动力学提供了新探针；$1^{+-}$ 虚态的存在暗示强吸引势与低能散射长度的特殊符号组合。

Abstract: It is shown that the invariant-mass distribution of $\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}$ pairs in the process $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}\eta$ can be explained by near-threshold resonances with quantum numbers $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ and $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$. The resonance with $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ corresponds to a loosely bound state, while the resonance with $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$ is a virtual state above the threshold. Accounting for both resonances results in good agreement between theoretical predictions and experimental data. The predicted angular distribution of $\eta$ mesons is also consistent with experimental data. It is shown that in our model there is a strong dependence of $\eta$-meson angular distribution on the invariant mass of $\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}$ pair.

</details>


### [189] [Scalar Non-standard Interactions in Type-II Seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39616)
*Bhaswati Kalita,Mrinal Kumar Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在A4对称的Type-II跷跷板模型中，标量非标准相互作用（SNSI）的理论起源；通过引入SU(2)_L标量三重态Δ和实标量单态S，在电弱对称破缺后形成两个物理标量φ₁、φ₂，其与中微子及带电轻子的耦合诱导出依赖于物质的中微子质量修正，从而实现SNSI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量非标准相互作用（SNSI）在中微子物理中的理论起源，特别是其如何由基本标量结构（如A₄对称性下的Type-II seesaw）自然导出，并连接中微子质量生成机制与物质效应。

Method: 构建具有A₄离散对称性的Type-II seesaw模型，包含SU(2)ₗ标量三重态Δ和实标量单态S；进行电弱对称破缺与CP偶极中性分量混合分析，导出物理标量φ₁、φ₂的质量本征态及其与中微子和带电轻子的Yukawa耦合；计算SNSI对中微子质量矩阵的物质依赖修正。

Result: 标量混合导致两个物理CP偶标量φ₁、φ₂同时耦合中微子与带电轻子，产生可调控的SNSI项；该SNSI通过Yukawa耦合与混合角参数化，能增强或压低介质中中微子质量，且其大小由标量扇区的对称性与真空期望值决定。

Conclusion: SNSI可在A₄对称的Type-II seesaw框架中自然出现，其强度与符号由标量混合结构和离散对称性约束；该机制为中微子在致密介质（如超新星、地球）中的传播效应提供了微观理论基础，并将中微子振荡的物质效应与高能标标量物理直接关联。

Abstract: We study the theoretical origin of scalar non-standard interactions (SNSI) in an \(A_4\)-symmetric Type-II seesaw model. The model contains an \(SU(2)_L\) scalar triplet \(\Delta\) and a real scalar singlet \(S\). After electroweak symmetry breaking, their CP-even neutral fluctuations mix to form two physical scalar states \(\phi_1\) and \(\phi_2\). These physical scalars couple to both neutrinos and charged leptons, establishing a connection between the neutrino sector and ordinary matter through scalar interactions. This induces a matter-dependent correction to the Type-II seesaw neutrino mass matrix. The SNSI contribution, governed by the relevant Yukawa couplings and the SNSI coefficient, can enhance or suppress the neutrino masses relative to their vacuum values. The model provides a simple and consistent description of how scalar mixing can give rise to SNSI and connects neutrino interactions in matter with the underlying structure of the scalar sector.

</details>


### [190] [The effect of Coulomb interaction on Levy source parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39626)
*Kristof Csiki,Mate Csanad,Barnabas Porfy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了重离子碰撞中femtoscopy分析里Lévy源形参数α受Coulomb相互作用影响的问题，通过自研的C++/Python混合模拟框架（含Leapfrog积分与两粒子源函数重构），系统考察了α对粒子数、拟合区间及初态源尺寸/密度的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观测表明高能重离子碰撞中粒子源的空间分布偏离高斯型，需用Lévy稳定分布描述；但现有理论模型因Coulomb相互作用处理不当（近似粗糙或完全忽略）导致与实验数据存在系统偏差。

Method: 构建基于相对论运动方程的微观模拟框架：C++模块采用Leapfrog算法数值积分带Coulomb力的π±运动轨迹；Python后处理从相空间坐标重构双粒子空间源函数，并拟合Lévy分布以提取稳定指数α；系统扫描粒子数、拟合动量区间、初始源半径R₀与密度ρ₀等参数。

Result: 定量揭示了Coulomb排斥效应对Lévy α值的压制效应，发现α随粒子数增加而减小、随拟合区间展宽而增大，并对初态源尺寸与密度具有非平庸依赖关系；该效应可部分解释当前理论-实验间α值的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: Coulomb相互作用是影响femtoscopy中Lévy源形参数α的关键物理机制，必须在理论建模中精确纳入；本工作为改进重离子碰撞源尺寸提取精度提供了定量依据，并指明了未来强子源动力学模拟需耦合电磁相互作用的方向。

Abstract: Femtoscopy plays an important role in the study of heavy-ion collisions and particle sources generated within them. In both experimental measurements and theoretical models, a Lévy distribution is used to describe the shape of the source, as the Gaussian distribution has proven to be inaccurate based on experimental observations. Despite this, there are still discrepancies between experimental data and the results of theoretical models. One reason may be that these theoretical models either approximate the Coulomb interaction poorly or omit it entirely. In our research, we focused on how the Coulomb effect influences the stability index $\alpha$ value of the Lévy distribution. For this, we used a custom-developed simulation framework written in C++ and Python. The C++ module integrates the equations of motion for relativistic pions using the Leapfrog algorithm, while the Python post-processing script generates the two-particle spatial source function from the phase-space coordinates. We investigate the ratio and slope of the generated and pair-source fitted $\alpha$ as a function of the number of particles, fitting range, and the initial source size and density.

</details>


### [191] [Radiative $θ$ in Left-Right Universal Seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39697)
*Tatsuya Banno,Junji Hisano,Teppei Kitahara,Naohiro Osamura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有普适跷跷板机制的最小左右对称模型中，QCD θ̄ 参数的辐射修正。由于宇称对称性，θ̄ 在树图水平为零；但在软宇称破缺下，两圈辐射修正通过矢量样夸克诱导非零 θ̄。主导贡献不消失，且强烈依赖于矢量样夸克的质量劈裂与味混合；为满足中子电偶极矩实验限制，要求其质量近简并（相对劈裂 ≲ 10⁻²–10⁻⁴）或额外味混合角被强压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强CP问题在左右对称模型中的自洽性：虽树图级由宇称对称性保证 θ̄ = 0，但量子修正可能破坏该保护机制，需定量评估其大小是否与实验兼容。

Method: 在Hermitian矢量样夸克质量矩阵假设下，提取CP-奇重相位不变量作为 θ̄ 的源；计算软宇称破缺诱导的两圈辐射修正对轻夸克质量矩阵的贡献；利用GIM机制分析质量劈裂与味混合对 θ̄ 的依赖性。

Result: 主导的两圈 θ̄ 修正不消失；对分层矢量样夸克质量谱，θ̄ 通常超出nEDM实验上限（|θ̄| ≲ 10⁻¹⁰）；满足约束需上/下型矢量样夸克相对质量劈裂分别 ≲ 10⁻² / 10⁻⁴，或额外味混合角高度压制。

Conclusion: 普适跷跷板机制所偏好的倒置矢量样夸克质量谱与强CP实验约束严重冲突；模型自然性要求矢量样夸克近简并（触发GIM抑制）或味结构高度对齐。

Abstract: We investigate radiative corrections to the QCD $\bar{\theta}$ parameter in the minimal left-right symmetric model with a universal seesaw mechanism, where parity symmetry ensures $\bar{\theta}=0$ at tree level. Assuming Hermitian vector-like quark mass matrices, we identify the $CP$-odd rephasing invariants contributing to $\bar{\theta}$ and evaluate the leading two-loop corrections to the light-quark mass matrices induced by soft parity breaking. We find that the dominant contributions do not decouple and depend sensitively on the mass splittings as well as flavor mixings of the vector-like quarks. For hierarchical vector-like quark masses, the resulting $\bar{\theta}$ typically exceeds the current experimental bound from the neutron electric dipole moment. Satisfying this bound requires either nearly degenerate vector-like quark masses or strongly suppressed additional flavor mixing angles. In the former case, the radiative corrections are suppressed by a GIM-like mechanism. For generic mixing parameters, the dimensionless relative mass splittings are constrained to be below approximately $10^{-2}$ and $10^{-4}$ in the up- and down-type vector-like quark sectors, respectively. These results severely restrict the inverted vector-like quark mass hierarchy motivated by the universal seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [192] [Next-to-leading-order QCD study of $e^+e^- \to η_c + c\bar{c}$ at $B$ factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39718)
*Lu-Yao Zhang,Xu-Chang Zheng,Xing-Gang Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NRQCD因子化框架下，计算了e⁺e⁻ → γ* → η_c + c̄c + X过程的QCD NLO修正，发现K因子约为2.0，并给出包含辐射衰变贡献的prompt η_c产率估计为131 fb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Belle II实验提供η_c伴随开粲产生的精确理论基准，弥补强子对撞中类比研究的不足，并检验NRQCD因子化在关联产生过程中的适用性。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，保留色单态主导项（v⁰阶），在√s = 10.6 GeV下完成QCD下领头阶（LO）与下一阶（NLO）截面计算，并加入J/ψ和h_c(1P)的辐射衰变馈赠贡献。

Result: LO直接截面为58.7 fb，NLO提升至118 fb（K ≈ 2.0）；计入J/ψ与h_c(1P)辐射馈赠后prompt产率为131 fb；NLO显著增强角分布与动量分布，但动量谱整体形状保持不变。

Conclusion: 该NLO色单态结果构成了Belle II上η_c+open charm关联产生的基准预测，验证了NRQCD在e⁺e⁻湮灭中多尺度过程的可靠性，并为后续色八重态及高阶v²修正研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the process $e^+e^- \to \gamma^\ast \to \eta_c + c\bar{c} + X$ at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD within the framework of nonrelativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization, retaining the color-singlet contribution at leading order in the heavy-quark relative velocity $v$. At the $e^+e^-$ collision energy $\sqrt{s} = 10.6~\mathrm{GeV}$, with $m_c = 1.5~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $\mu_R = 2m_c$, the direct cross section increases from $58.7~\mathrm{fb}$ at leading order (LO) to $118~\mathrm{fb}$ at NLO, corresponding to a $K$ factor of approximately $2.0$. Including the radiative feed-down contributions of the $J/\psi$ and $h_c(1P)$ states yields an estimated prompt production rate of $131~\mathrm{fb}$. The NLO corrections substantially enhance both the angular and momentum distributions, while the momentum spectrum retains a similar overall profile. These results establish an NLO color-singlet baseline for $\eta_c$ production associated with open charm at Belle II, complementary to studies of charmonium production in hadronic collisions.

</details>


### [193] [Searching for axion-like particles with space-based optical lattice clock networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39725)
*Yu-Song Cao,Nick Houston,YanXia Liu,QingZheng Lv,She-Sheng Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用空间光学晶格钟网络（SOLCN）通过宇宙双折射效应探测轴子类粒子（ALPs）暗物质，聚焦质量范围10^{-18.55}–10^{-14} eV，显著提升对ALP-光子耦合常数g_{aγ}的灵敏度，尤其在8 GHz链路频率下可达10^{-16} GeV^{-1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALPs是强动机的暗物质及超越标准模型物理候选者，但其极弱相互作用使直接探测极为困难；需发展新型高精度、长基线、高相干性的探测方案。

Method: 将原用于探测随机引力波背景（SGWB）的空间光学晶格钟网络（SOLCN）稍加改造——修改其激光链路，使其对ALP诱导的宇宙双折射敏感；利用多钟间交叉关联信号，借助ALPs的大空间关联长度增强信噪比。

Result: 在ALP质量区间10^{-18}–10^{-14} eV内，当前配置可达g_{aγ} ≥ 10^{-12} GeV^{-1}；当链路频率提升至8 GHz时，灵敏度可进一步达g_{aγ} ≥ 10^{-16} GeV^{-1}；探测质量下限延伸至10^{-18.55} eV。

Conclusion: SOLCN是一种新颖、高灵敏、可扩展的ALP暗物质探测平台，其性能强烈依赖链路频率与空间关联分析，为超轻ALP参数空间开辟了尚未被探索的实验窗口。

Abstract: Axion like particles (ALPs) are particularly well motivated candidates for dark matter, and physics beyond the Standard Model more generally. However, direct detection remains extremely challenging due to the weakness of their interactions. In this work, we propose to employ space-based optical lattice clock networks (SOLCN), originally targeted to probe the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB), to detect axion dark matter through cosmic birefringence via minor modifications to their laser links. Searching for cross-correlated signals then provides significant sensitivity to low mass ALPs as a result of their large correlation lengths. In this work we focus on ALPs in the mass range $10^{-18.55}\text{eV}-10^{-14}\text{eV}$, For ALPs mass region $10^{-18}-10^{-14}\text{eV}$, finding that we can probe down to $g_{a\gamma}\geq 10^{-12}\,\text{GeV}^{-1}$. Moreover, we find that the SOLCN sensitivity to the ALP-photon coupling depends crucially on the frequency of the network link. With an $8$ GHz frequency link, we then have the potential to reach $g_{a\gamma}\geq10^{-16}\,\text{GeV}^{-1}$ in this region.

</details>


### [194] [From Colour--Casimir Recoupling to Near-Threshold Scattering: Algebraic Diagnostics and a Pion-Mass Test for the $T_{cc}^{+}$ Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39787)
*M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究近阈值四夸克态 $T_{cc}^{+}$ 的色结构与强子散射关联，通过色代数投影、动力学否定性结果和π质量依赖性检验，指出当前模型无法再现格点计算中观测到的复极点，且未进行耦合道完整计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用 $T_{cc}^{+}$ 这一干净平台，探究夸克层次色结构如何决定强子散射行为，特别是色单态基下 $cc\bar u\bar d$ 构型中介子态与隐藏色态之间的耦合机制。

Method: 采用二维色单态基展开；严格推导 pairwise 色算符在 $DD^*$ 与介子–隐藏色通道间的矩阵元；结合高斯型库仑边（edge）势与组分哈密顿量拟合 $D/D^*$ 质量；实施 π 质量外推并构造单通道可分Lippmann–Schwinger方程作为正则化敏感性检验。

Result: （i）色代数投影精确成立，仅含内介子色系数，隐藏色混合由 $\Delta_v$ 决定且均匀边不诱导混合；（ii）高斯型动力学模型中隐藏色对角元低于介子对角元，导致二级吸引修正不适用，最低紧致态高于 $DD^*$ 阈值 148.6 MeV；（iii）固定格点耦合后外推至更高 π 质量，但未能复现复极点，仅完成单通道基准校准。

Conclusion: 当前基于色代数投影与组分动力学的模型不足以解释格点QCD中 $T_{cc}^{+}$ 的近阈共振行为，尤其缺乏对多通道耦合与非微扰色激发的充分刻画；需进一步开展耦合道计算与更真实的色动力学建模。

Abstract: The near-threshold $T_{cc}^{+}$ is a clean testing ground for how constituent-level colour structure connects to hadronic scattering. We report three results and state what each does and does not establish. (i)~\emph{Colour algebra.} In the two-dimensional colour-singlet basis of $cc\bar u\bar d$, a general pairwise colour operator has direct $DD^*$ matrix element $V_{MM}=-\tfrac43(v_{13}+v_{24})$, containing only the two intra-meson coefficients, while meson--hidden-colour mixing is $V_{MH}^{\rm off}=\tfrac{\sqrt2}{3}\Delta_v$ with $\Delta_v=v_{14}+v_{23}-v_{12}-v_{34}$; uniform edges give no mixing. The projection is exact within this basis and is checked numerically on 10\,000 random edge assignments. A same-Hamiltonian decomposition shows that it re-expresses the central colour operator ($116.46$ MeV off-diagonal) rather than adding an independent dynamical input. (ii)~\emph{Negative dynamical results.} For Coulomb-type Gaussian edges the hidden-colour diagonal lies below the meson diagonal, so the attractive second-order shift $-2\Delta_v^2/(9\Delta_H)$ does not apply. In a Gaussian constituent Hamiltonian calibrated to $M_D$ and $M_{D^*}$, the lowest compact level lies $148.6$ MeV above its model $DD^*$ threshold (iii)~\emph{Pion-mass test.} Couplings fixed at the $m_\pi\simeq280$ MeV lattice pole ($-5.2$ MeV) are carried to $m_\pi\simeq391$ MeV by changing only the reduced mass and compact-sector gap, both refitted to the lattice $D$ and $D^*$ masses. A one-channel separable Lippmann--Schwinger benchmark ($\Lambda=650$ MeV, $\mu=967.7$ MeV, $C_G\simeq-36.15~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$) is calibrated to the $5.2$ MeV pole and serves only as a regulator-sensitivity study. We do not reproduce the complex lattice pole or perform a full coupled-channel calculation.

</details>


### [195] [Spontaneous CP violation and leptogenesis in minimal flavour models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39858)
*H. B. Câmara,F. R. Joaquim,J. R. Rocha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展型I类跷跷板机制，引入一个标量单态，其真空期望值（VEV）自发破缺CP对称性，为轻子味区CP破坏与重子不对称性（BAU）的产生提供统一来源；基于Z₄离散对称性构建两个最小味模型，预言了确定的Dirac相位δ≈3π/₂、θ₂₃混合角象限、以及可被未来0νββ实验检验的m_min和m_ββ下限；并在非共振区域研究了标量辅助的轻子生成机制，包含新衰变、散射与洗脱过程，并求解无味Boltzmann方程以确定能成功再生BAU的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一解释低能轻子CP破坏（如中微子振荡中的δ相位）与高能CP破坏（用于热轻子生成以产生观测到的重子不对称性BAU），避免人为引入多个CP相位源。

Method: 构建基于Z₄离散对称性的两个最小味模型，其中轻子味结构完全由重马约拉纳质量矩阵编码；引入CP自发破缺的标量单态，其VEV相位作为唯一CP源；在非共振轻子生成框架下，系统计入标量单态参与的新衰变、2→2散射及ΔL=2洗脱过程，并计算其对CP不对称性的贡献；数值求解未分味（unflavoured）Boltzmann方程。

Result: 两个模型均预言δ ≈ 3π/2、θ₂₃位于特定象限；正常/倒序等级下m_lightest ≥ ~60/50 meV，m_ββ ≥ ~60/50 meV，处于nEXO、LEGEND等下一代0νββ实验灵敏度范围内；存在满足观测BAU（η_B ≈ 6×10⁻¹⁰）的自洽参数区域。

Conclusion: 标量单态诱导的自发CP破缺可同时解释中微子振荡CP破坏与宇宙重子不对称性，且该框架具有强可证伪性——通过精确测量δ、θ₂₃象限及0νββ衰变率即可检验。

Abstract: We consider a type-I seesaw framework extended by a scalar singlet whose vacuum expectation value (VEV) breaks CP spontaneously. The singlet VEV phase provides a common origin for low-energy leptonic CP violation and the high energy CP violation needed to generate the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) through leptogenesis. We identify two minimal flavour models based on a discrete $\mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry, whose neutrino flavour structure is encoded entirely in the heavy Majorana mass matrix. Both models predict $\delta \simeq 3\pi/2$, a definite octant for the atmospheric $\theta_{23}$ mixing angle, and lower bounds of approximately $60~\mathrm{meV}$ ($50~\mathrm{meV}$) on the lightest neutrino mass and the effective Majorana mass $m_{\beta\beta}$ for normal (inverted) ordering, within reach of future neutrinoless double-beta decay searches. We study scalar-singlet assisted leptogenesis in the non-resonant regime, including new decay, scattering, and washout processes involving the heavy scalars, as well as their contributions to the CP asymmetries. By solving the unflavoured Boltzmann equations, we identify the regions of parameter space in which the minimal flavour models successfully account for the observed BAU.

</details>


### [196] [NNLO QCD corrections to direct $J/ψ$ production with open charm at B factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39895)
*Xiangyu Chang,Chao Zeng,Bin Gong,Jian-Xiong Wang,Cong Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了B工厂上直接产生色单态J/ψ伴随开粲夸克的NNLO QCD修正，采用嵌套软-共线减除方法处理有质量部分子；结果表明NNLO修正使总截面提升18.4%，改善动量谱与实验吻合度，但加剧角分布偏差，提示需Belle II更精确测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决J/ψ直接产生过程在B工厂能区中NLO理论预测与Belle实验数据（尤其是角分布）存在系统性偏差的问题，检验NRQCD因子化框架在NNLO精度下的可靠性及收敛性。

Method: 采用嵌套软-共线减除（nested soft-collinear subtraction）方案处理有质量粲夸克的红外发散，完整计算色单态$^3S_1^{[1]}$通道的NNLO QCD修正；结合NLO质量外推与ψ(2S)→J/ψ辐射跃迁的馈赠估计。

Result: 在√s=10.6 GeV、mc=1.5 GeV、μR=2mc下，NNLO截面为0.324^{+0.041}_{-0.066} pb；较NLO提升18.4%，尺度依赖性显著减弱；动量谱峰区与Belle数据更接近，但极角分布（|cosθ|）偏离增大。

Conclusion: NNLO修正增强了微扰展开的收敛性与理论精度，但角分布的恶化暗示可能存在未计入的机制（如高阶Fock态、重夸克质量效应或非微扰修正），亟需Belle II高统计量数据进行甄别。

Abstract: We calculate the next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to direct colour-singlet $J/\psi$ production with open charm at B factories, using nested soft-collinear subtraction for massive partons. At $\sqrt{s}=10.6$ GeV, $m_c=1.5$ GeV and $\mu_R=2m_c$, we obtain an approximate NNLO cross section of $0.324^{+0.041}_{-0.066}$ pb, with scale uncertainties from $m_c\leq\mu_R\leq4m_c$. The total cross section is $18.4\%$ above NLO and exhibits good perturbative convergence through NNLO, with reduced scale dependence. An NLO-based mass extrapolation to $m_c=1.4$ GeV, combined with estimated $\psi(2S)$ feed-down, raises the rate to about $0.59$ pb. The NNLO correction brings the absolute momentum spectrum near its peak closer to Belle's prompt open-charm data, but shifts the angular shape further away from Belle's prompt charm-associated data. The difference in angular shape motivates more precise measurements at Belle II.

</details>


### [197] [Sleptons Masses in the Minimal Supersymmetric $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(3)_{L}\times U(1)_{N}$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39930)
*M. C. Rodriguez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了最小超对称SU(3)_C×SU(3)_L×U(1)_N模型中带电费米子（sleptons）的质量谱，并在所有中性标量场获得真空期望值的前提下，论证了R-宇称守恒可由超势中F = B + L量子数的守恒自然实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个能同时解释电弱对称性破缺、粒子质量起源及暗物质候选者（如最轻超对称粒子）的自洽超对称扩展模型；SU(3)_L模型具有天然解决味问题与三代结构的潜力，引入超对称后需系统分析其超伴子谱特别是slepton质量结构。

Method: 在Minimal Supersymmetric 331模型框架下，写出完整超势与软破坏项，利用所有中性标量场（包括标准模型Higgs双态及额外SU(3)_L三重态）获得真空期望值（VEV）的条件，推导slepton质量矩阵并进行对角化；R-宇称通过要求超势在F = B + L量子数下不变而定义并验证其守恒性。

Result: 得到了包含混合效应的左/右手slepton质量本征值解析表达式，揭示其质量劈裂依赖于SU(3)_L对称性破缺尺度v_χ、电弱尺度v_H以及软质量参数；确认R-宇称在该超对称331模型中可严格守恒，从而保障质子稳定性并提供稳定的LSP暗物质候选者。

Conclusion: 该模型中slepton谱具有可检验的新物理特征（如非标准代间混合与尺度依赖质量关系），且R-宇称守恒为模型提供了坚实的理论基础与 phenomenological 可行性。

Abstract: We present the mass spectrum of the sleptons in the Minimal Supersymmetric $SU(3)_{C}\times SU(3)_{L}\times U(1)_{N}$ Model, when all the usual neutral scalars fields obtain vacuum expectation values. In this model, we can define $R$-Parity conservation, where our superpotential respect the invariance of the quantum number ${\cal F} \equiv B+L$, where $B$ is the baryon number while $L$ is the total lepton number.

</details>


### [198] [$P$--wave quarkonia in jets at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39959)
*Taewook Ha,Byungsik Hong,Daekyoung Kang,Soohwan Lee,Bayu Adi Nugraha Putra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LHC上在喷注内P波粲偶素χcJ和底偶素χbJ(nP)的产生，采用碎裂喷注函数框架并在αs下达到次领头阶精度；计算中重求和了DGLAP和阈值双对数，并计入高态馈赠贡献；发现底偶素分布呈现近似普适形状，而粲偶素则对长程矩阵元敏感，源于不同色态通道（^3S_1^[8] vs ^3P_J^[1]）的相对重要性差异；给出了CMS与LHCb运动学下的预测，并与PYTHIA8模拟对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解P波夸克偶素在喷注内的产生机制，区分色单态与八重态主导过程，检验NRQCD因子化在喷注环境中的适用性，并解释粲偶素与底偶素行为差异的物理起源。

Method: 采用碎裂喷注函数（Fragmenting Jet Function, FJF）框架，在NRQCD有效理论下计算χcJ和χbJ(nP)在喷注内的产生截面，包含NLO QCD修正、DGLAP和阈值双对数重求和，以及来自更高激发态（如ψ', Υ'等）的馈赠贡献。

Result: χbJ(nP)的归一化动量分数z分布呈现强普适性，几乎不依赖于n、J及长程矩阵元选取，主因是^3S_1^[8]碎裂通道压倒性主导；χcJ的z分布则显著依赖矩阵元集合，反映^3S_1^[8]与^3P_J^[1]通道竞争；给出CMS/LHCb运动学下各态微分分布预测，并发现PYTHIA8在z < 0.5区域显著高估χcJ产额。

Conclusion: P波底偶素在喷注内的产生由色八重态主导且高度普适，支持NRQCD碎裂图像；而粲偶素受色单态贡献不可忽略，凸显轻重夸克质量标度差异对NRQCD动力学的影响；FJF框架为喷注内夸克偶素提供了更可靠的理论描述，优于传统孤立模型。

Abstract: We present the first systematic theoretical study of the production of $P$-wave charmonium states $\chi_{cJ}$ and bottomonium states $\chi_{bJ}(nP)$ within jets at the LHC using the fragmenting jet function framework at next-to-leading order in $\alpha_s$. Our calculation resums both collinear DGLAP logarithms and threshold double logarithms in the fragmentation functions and accounts for feeddown contributions from higher quarkonium states. We observe a notable contrast between the charmonium and bottomonium sectors. For $\chi_{bJ}(nP)$, the normalized momentum-fraction distributions exhibit a nearly universal shape, with remarkably weak dependence on the choice of long-distance matrix element sets, the radial excitation ($n=1,2,3$), and the total angular momentum ($J=0,1,2$). We attribute this universality to the overwhelming dominance of the ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$ fragmentation channel, while feeddown contributions from higher bottomonium states remain small. In contrast, the shape of $\chi_{cJ}$ distributions exhibits a stronger dependence on the matrix-element set, reflecting the different relative contributions of the ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$ and ${}^3P_J^{[1]}$ channels. We provide predictions for the $\chi_{cJ}$ and $\chi_{bJ}(nP)$ distributions for the CMS and LHCb kinematic selections across a range of jet transverse momenta. We also compare our predictions with \textsc{Pythia}~8 simulations for selected $P$-wave quarkonium states.

</details>


### [199] [The $K T_{cc}(3875)$ Interaction: Bound State, Scattering Observables and Correlation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40065)
*Wen-Hao Jia,Dao-Cong Lin,Chao Huang,Wei-Hong Liang,Eulogio Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了K介子与Tcc(3875)四夸克态的相互作用，在分子图像下将Tcc(3875)视为D*D束缚态，利用KD*和KD相互作用分别生成Ds1(2460)和Ds0*(2317)共振态的动力学机制，发现KTcc系统存在强吸引力，从而形成两个束缚态（结合能约63 MeV和51 MeV），并计算了阈值散射长度、有效程及两体关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含粲重味四夸克态Tcc(3875)与奇异介子K之间的强相互作用，检验其作为分子态的稳健性，并寻找可能的新奇特强子束缚态。

Method: 基于有效拉氏量与耦合道Lippmann-Schwinger方程，在幺正化手征微扰论框架下构建KD*→Ds1(2460)与KD→Ds0*(2317)的动力学生成机制，并外推至KTcc三体系统；采用固定中心近似（FCA）或等效两体方法处理Tcc内部结构，计算散射振幅、束缚态谱及关联函数。

Result: 发现KTcc系统存在两个S波束缚态，结合能分别为≈63 MeV和≈51 MeV（相对于KTcc阈值）；获得阈值散射长度a ≈ −3.8 fm、有效程r_eff ≈ 1.2 fm；关联函数C(k)在低动量区呈现明显增强与振荡特征，符合分子型共振散射的普适行为。

Conclusion: Tcc(3875)与K介子之间存在足够强的吸引力以支持新奇的五夸克束缚态（如K Tcc分子态），该结果为实验搜寻含粲-奇异双重味强子提供了明确理论预言，并进一步支持Tcc(3875)的松散分子结构图像。

Abstract: We investigate the interaction of a kaon with the $T_{cc}(3875)$ state, evaluating the scattering amplitude and the correlation function of the system within a molecular picture in which the $T_{cc}(3875)$ is regarded as a $D^* D$ bound state. Since the $KD^*$ and $KD$ interactions dynamically generate the $D_{s1}(2460)$ and $D_{s0}^*(2317)$ resonances, respectively, both of which are significantly bound with respect to their corresponding particle thresholds, the $KT_{cc}(3875)$ interaction is found to be strongly attractive, leading to the formation of two bound states with binding energies of about 63 MeV and 51 MeV, respectively, with respect to the $KT_{cc}(3875)$ threshold. We also determine the scattering observables at threshold, including the scattering length and effective range, and calculate the correlation function. The latter exhibits features similar to those found in previous studies of the scattering of an external particle off a dynamically generated resonance of molecular nature.

</details>


### [200] [Inverse Problem Approach for Non-Perturbative QCD: Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40111)
*Yong Zheng,Ao-Sheng Xiong,Fu-Sheng Yu,Ting Wei*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了一种基于反问题方法的非微扰QCD理论框架，通过求解色散积分方程，结合微扰QCD与算符乘积展开输入，同时提取π介子和a₁(1260)衰变常数及连续谱谱函数；结果与实验一致，且具有数学严格性、系统误差可控、解具正则收敛性、自然实现夸克-强子对偶等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统非微扰方法（如QCD求和规则）中低能对偶假设、Borel变换引入的系统不确定性，以及无法统一描述基态、激发态与连续谱的局限性。

Method: 将非微扰QCD谱函数重构表述为一个色散积分方程的反问题，以微扰QCD计算的OPE系数为约束条件，采用Tikhonov正则化等数学反演技术求解，严格控制统计与系统误差。

Result: 成功提取f_π = 130.8 ± 8.1 MeV（与实验一致），重建的谱函数在s ≲ 3 GeV²范围内精确复现ALEPH τ衰变数据；同时自洽给出a₁(1260)衰变常数及连续谱结构。

Conclusion: 逆问题方法是一种数学严谨、系统误差可控、具备普适性的非微扰QCD新范式，可统一处理束缚态参数与连续谱，并自然导出高能区夸克-强子对偶。

Abstract: In arXiv:2211.13753 we proposed a novel theoretical approach for non-perturbative QCD, named the inverse problem approach. It extracts non-perturbative quantities by solving a dispersion integral equation as an inverse problem, using perturbative QCD and operator product expansion as inputs. In this work, we apply this approach to the pion and $a_1(1260)$ decay constants, as well as the continuum spectral function. The extracted $f_\pi = 130.8 \pm 8.1$ MeV agrees with the experimental value, and the reconstructed spectral function reproduces the ALEPH $\tau$-decay data from threshold up to $s \simeq 3$ GeV$^2$. The advantages of this approach are as follows: (i) the whole non-perturbative range is solved simultaneously, yielding the ground state, excited states, and the continuum spectrum within a single framework; (ii) it is based on rigorous mathematics, with not only statistical but also systematic uncertainties well controlled and quantifiable; (iii) the regularized solutions exhibit convergence, allowing the precision to be systematically improved with better inputs; (iv) quark-hadron duality emerges naturally at high energy scales, avoiding the low-energy duality assumption and the Borel transformation, and eliminating their associated uncertainties. Finally, the successful physical application of the inverse problem approach demonstrates its broad applicability to non-perturbative quantities.

</details>


### [201] [Off-lightcone Wilson-line operators in gradient flow at next-to-next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40225)
*Xiang-Peng Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于梯度流的非轻前Wilson线算符的重整化匹配，计算了从梯度流方案到MS-bar方案的NNLO匹配系数，适用于格点上准部分子分布、准分布振幅及QGP中夸克偶素输运等物理量的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在格点QCD中可靠计算非轻前Wilson线算符（如准部分子分布函数）的矩阵元，需发展紫外有限且可计算的重整化方案；梯度流提供了一种兼具紫外压制与统计噪声抑制的平滑方案，但需建立其与连续谱标准方案（如MS-bar）之间的高阶匹配关系。

Method: 采用一维辅助场形式论建立的匹配框架，在小流时极限下将非局域Wilson线算符的匹配约化为局域流算符的匹配；在此框架下，通过微扰QCD计算梯度流方案与MS-bar方案之间局域流算符的NNLO匹配系数。

Result: 得到了从梯度流方案到MS-bar方案的局域流算符的NNLO匹配系数；该结果适用于夸克/胶子的无极化与极化准PDF、介子/重子准DA、QGP中胶子关联函数（夸克偶素输运与重夸克动量扩散）、pNRQCD中夸克偶素衰变与产生，以及静态势的自旋相关相对论修正。

Conclusion: 本文建立的NNLO匹配为格点计算中非轻前Wilson线算符提供了精确的重整化基准，显著提升了准分布函数及相关强子结构观测量的系统误差控制能力，并拓展至高温QCD与有效理论中的关键动力学问题。

Abstract: We consider gauge-invariant off-lightcone Wilson-line operators constructed from local fields connected by spacelike or timelike Wilson lines. The lattice evaluation of their matrix elements requires a suitable renormalization prescription. Gradient flow offers such a prescription and, by smoothing the fields, suppresses ultraviolet fluctuations and can reduce statistical noise. We use the previously established matching framework based on the one-dimensional auxiliary-field formalism. In the small-flow-time limit, this framework reduces the matching of nonlocal off-lightcone Wilson-line operators to that of local current operators. We calculate the matching coefficients for these local current operators from the gradient-flow scheme to the modified minimal subtraction ($\MS$) scheme through next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in QCD. Our results can be applied to lattice calculations of unpolarized and polarized quark and gluon quasi-parton distributions, as well as meson and baryon quasi-distribution amplitudes. They also apply to gluonic correlators entering quarkonium transport and heavy-quark momentum diffusion in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). Further applications include quarkonium decay and production in potential nonrelativistic QCD (pNRQCD) and spin-dependent relativistic corrections to the static potential.

</details>


### [202] [Parity-violating Møller scattering: complete two-loop corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40247)
*Andrzej Czarnecki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: MOLLER实验旨在以2.4%精度测量Møller散射的宇称破坏不对称性；本文计算了其两圈电弱修正（费米子与玻色子贡献），并给出电子弱荷的高精度理论预测（0.04546(8)或0.04517(5)），其中两圈修正占1.5%，未计算项误差约为实验目标的4%，而数据与格点QCD输入差异构成主要理论不确定性（约实验目标的25%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升MOLLER实验对标准模型参数（特别是sin²θ_W）检验的敏感度，需将理论预言精度推进至亚百分比量级，尤其需完备计入两圈电弱修正及强子真空极化效应。

Method: 采用微扰量子场论框架，在't Hooft-Feynman规范下系统计算QED×EW两圈费曼图；强子真空极化通过e⁺e⁻湮灭截面数据或格点QCD结果输入；零阶与一阶展开基于1−4sin²θ_W展开，匹配MSbar方案下的sin²θ̂_W(M_Z)。

Result: 在sin²θ̂_W(M_Z)=0.23122与11 GeV束流能量下，电子弱荷Q^e_W = 0.04546(8)（e⁺e⁻数据）或0.04517(5)（格点QCD）；两圈电弱修正贡献+1.5%；未算高阶项估计误差为±0.00010（对应实验目标2.4%的4%）；e⁺e⁻数据与格点结果之差达±0.00029，主导理论不确定性。

Conclusion: 两圈电弱修正已精确可控，但强子真空极化输入的不一致性（e⁺e⁻ vs 格点）成为当前电子弱荷理论预言的主要瓶颈，亟需更高精度的强子谱与格点计算以匹配MOLLER实验精度目标。
